</button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Field({ label, hint, children }: { label: string; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <div className=\"flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800\">{label}</div>\n        {hint ? <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">{hint}</div> : null}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"mt-1\">{children}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction OverlayRightDrawer({\n  title,\n  open,\n  onClose,\n  children,\n  width = 420,\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  width?: number;\n}) {\n  if (!open) return null;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed inset-0 z-40\">\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/20\" onClick={onClose} />\n      <div className=\"absolute right-0 top-0 h-full bg-white shadow-xl border-l\" style={{ width }} role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\">\n        <div className=\"h-14 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n          <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{title}</div>\n          <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"關閉\" ariaLabel=\"關閉\" onClick={() => onClose()}>\n            ✕\n          </Button>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"h-[calc(100%-56px)] overflow-auto p-4\">{children}</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction OverlayModal({\n  title,\n  open,\n  onClose,\n  children,\n  maxWidth = 860,\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  maxWidth?: number;\n}) {\n  if (!open) return null;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed inset-0 z-40\">\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/30\" onClick={onClose} />\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 p-6 grid place-items-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-full bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl border overflow-hidden\" style={{ maxWidth }} role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\">\n          <div className=\"h-14 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n            <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{title}</div>\n            <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"關閉\" ariaLabel=\"關閉\" onClick={onClose}>\n              ✕\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"p-4 max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto\">{children}</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// -----------------------\n// Main component\n// -----------------------\n\nexport default function App() {\n  // Layout widths (persist)\n  const [leftW, setLeftW] = useLocalStorageState<number>(\"pmw:leftW\", 340);\n  const [midW, setMidW] = useLocalStorageState<number>(\"pmw:midW\", 460);\n\n  // Global behaviors\n  const [pinList, setPinList] = useLocalStorageState<boolean>(\"pmw:pinList\", false);\n\n  // UI state\n  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string>(\"p1\");\n  const [listVisible, setListVisible] = useState<boolean>(true);\n  const [focusMode, setFocusMode] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  const [snippetsOpen, setSnippetsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [metaDrawerOpen, setMetaDrawerOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [projectDetailsOpen, setProjectDetailsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [helpOpen, setHelpOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // Preface UI (per-prompt; default collapsed)\n  const [prefaceOpen, setPrefaceOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // Data state (in-memory)\n  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>(DEMO_PROJECTS);\n  const [promptsByProject, setPromptsByProject] = useState<Record<string, PromptItem[]>>(DEMO_PROMPTS);\n\n  const inboxId = \"__inbox__\";\n  const inboxCount = (promptsByProject[inboxId] ?? []).length;\n\n  const prompts = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n  const [selectedPromptId, setSelectedPromptId] = useState<string | null>(prompts[0]?.id ?? null);\n\n  const selectedProject = useMemo<Project | null>(() => projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId) ?? null, [projects, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Search/filter\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>(\"\");\n  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<string>(\"all\");\n  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>(\"all\");\n\n  // Undo snackbar\n  const [snackbar, setSnackbar] = useState<SnackbarState | null>(null);\n\n  // Editor refs\n  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);\n  const prefaceRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);\n\n  // Keep selection stable when switching project\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    if (!list.length) {\n      setSelectedPromptId(null);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!list.some((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId)) {\n      setSelectedPromptId(list[0].id);\n    }\n  }, [selectedProjectId, promptsByProject, selectedPromptId]);\n\n  const selectedPrompt = useMemo<PromptItem | null>(() => {\n    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    return list.find((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId) ?? null;\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, selectedPromptId]);\n\n  // Simulated save status\n  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<\"saved\" | \"dirty\" | \"saving\">(\"saved\");\n  const saveTimer = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  const setPromptBody = (newBody: string) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) =>\n        p.id === selectedPrompt.id\n          ? { ...p, body: newBody, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }\n          : p\n      );\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n\n    setSaveState(\"dirty\");\n    if (saveTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer.current);\n    saveTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setSaveState(\"saving\");\n      window.setTimeout(() => setSaveState(\"saved\"), 350);\n    }, 700);\n  };\n\n  const setPromptPreface = (newPreface: string) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) =>\n        p.id === selectedPrompt.id\n          ? { ...p, preface: newPreface, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }\n          : p\n      );\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n\n    setSaveState(\"dirty\");\n    if (saveTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer.current);\n    saveTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setSaveState(\"saving\");\n      window.setTimeout(() => setSaveState(\"saved\"), 350);\n    }, 700);\n  };\n\n  const setPromptFM = (patch: Partial<Frontmatter>) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) =>\n        p.id === selectedPrompt.id\n          ? { ...p, fm: { ...p.fm, ...patch, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }\n          : p\n      );\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n  };\n\n  // Filtering\n  const filteredPrompts = useMemo(() => {\n    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();\n    return (promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [])\n      .filter((p) => {\n        if (typeFilter !== \"all\" && p.fm.type !== typeFilter) return false;\n        if (statusFilter !== \"all\" && p.fm.status !== statusFilter) return false;\n        if (!q) return true;\n        const hay = `${p.fm.title} ${p.fm.note} ${(p.fm.tags || []).join(\" \")} ${p.body}`.toLowerCase();\n        return hay.includes(q);\n      })\n      .slice();\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, search, typeFilter, statusFilter]);\n\n  const uniqueTypes = useMemo(() => {\n    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.type).filter(Boolean));\n    return [\"all\", ...Array.from(set)];\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  const uniqueStatuses = useMemo(() => {\n    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.status).filter(Boolean));\n    return [\"all\", ...Array.from(set)];\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Pin OFF behavior: selecting prompt hides list\n  const selectPrompt = (promptId: string) => {\n    setSelectedPromptId(promptId);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n  };\n\n  // Click same project to reveal list (when hidden)\n  const onClickProject = (pid: string) => {\n    if (pid === selectedProjectId) {\n      if (!listVisible) setListVisible(true);\n      return;\n    }\n    setSelectedProjectId(pid);\n    setListVisible(true);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // Focus mode: hide middle list, keep left minimal\n  const toggleFocusMode = () => {\n    setFocusMode((v) => !v);\n    setListVisible(false);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // Helpers for overlays\n  const closeAllOverlays = () => {\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // CRUD actions\n  const createPrompt = ({ toInbox = false }: { toInbox?: boolean } = {}) => {\n    const id = nextId(toInbox ? \"in\" : \"pr\");\n    const projectName = toInbox ? \"收件匣\" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name;\n\n    const newPrompt: PromptItem = {\n      id,\n      fm: makeFrontmatter({\n        title: \"新草稿\",\n        project: toInbox ? \"收件匣\" : (projectName ?? \"\"),\n        note: toInbox ? \"inbox\" : \"\",\n      }),\n      preface: \"\",\n      body: \"\",\n    };\n\n    if (toInbox) {\n      setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n        const list = prev[inboxId] ?? [];\n        return { ...prev, [inboxId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n      });\n      setSelectedProjectId(inboxId);\n      setSelectedPromptId(id);\n      setPrefaceOpen(false);\n      setListVisible(pinList ? true : false);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增草稿到收件匣（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增提示詞（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const duplicatePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const id = nextId(\"dup\");\n    const copy: PromptItem = {\n      ...selectedPrompt,\n      id,\n      fm: {\n        ...selectedPrompt.fm,\n        title: `${selectedPrompt.fm.title}（複製）`,\n        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      },\n    };\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [copy, ...list] };\n    });\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已建立副本（另存為）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const archivePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);\n    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已歸檔：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const deletePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);\n    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已刪除：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const movePromptMock = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const target = projects.find((p) => p.id !== selectedProjectId);\n    if (!target) return;\n\n    const fromId = selectedProjectId;\n    const toId = target.id;\n    const moved = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[fromId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === moved.id);\n    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const fromList = (prev[fromId] ?? []).filter((p) => p.id !== moved.id);\n      const toList = prev[toId] ?? [];\n      const moved2: PromptItem = { ...moved, fm: { ...moved.fm, project: target.name, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } };\n      return { ...prev, [fromId]: fromList, [toId]: [moved2, ...toList] };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已移動到：${target.name}（示意）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const toList = (prev[toId] ?? []).filter((p) => p.id !== moved.id);\n          const fromList = prev[fromId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [toId]: toList, [fromId]: [moved, ...fromList] };\n        });\n        setSelectedProjectId(fromId);\n        setSelectedPromptId(moved.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const copyToClipboard = async () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const text = selectedPrompt.body;\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已複製到剪貼簿\", undo: null });\n    } catch {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"複製失敗：瀏覽器限制\", undo: null });\n    }\n  };\n\n  const insertSnippet = (snippetText: string) => {\n    const el = editorRef.current;\n    if (!el || !selectedPrompt) return;\n    const start = el.selectionStart ?? 0;\n    const end = el.selectionEnd ?? 0;\n    const before = selectedPrompt.body.slice(0, start);\n    const after = selectedPrompt.body.slice(end);\n    const nextText = before + snippetText + after;\n    setPromptBody(nextText);\n\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n      const pos = start + snippetText.length;\n      el.focus();\n      el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);\n    });\n  };\n\n  // Project details (edit fields)\n  const updateProject = (pid: string, patch: Partial<Project>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === pid ? { ...p, ...patch, updatedAt: p.updatedAt } : p)));\n  };\n\n  const addProjectLink = (pid: string) => {\n    const id = nextId(\"link\");\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) =>\n        p.id === pid\n          ? {\n              ...p,\n              links: [...(p.links ?? []), { id, title: \"新連結\", url: \"\" }],\n            }\n          : p\n      )\n    );\n  };\n\n  const updateProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string, patch: Partial<LinkItem>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).map((l) => (l.id === lid ? { ...l, ...patch } : l));\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  const removeProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).filter((l) => l.id !== lid);\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  // Resizer logic\n  const dragState = useRef<{ dragging: null | \"left\" | \"mid\"; startX: number; startLeft: number; startMid: number }>({\n    dragging: null,\n    startX: 0,\n    startLeft: 0,\n    startMid: 0,\n  });\n\n  const onPointerDownDivider = (which: \"left\" | \"mid\") => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    dragState.current.dragging = which;\n    dragState.current.startX = e.clientX;\n    dragState.current.startLeft = leftW;\n    dragState.current.startMid = midW;\n    if (e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture) e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n  };\n\n  const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n    const ds = dragState.current;\n    if (!ds.dragging) return;\n    const dx = e.clientX - ds.startX;\n\n    if (ds.dragging === \"left\") {\n      const next = Math.max(280, Math.min(460, ds.startLeft + dx));\n      setLeftW(next);\n    }\n\n    if (ds.dragging === \"mid\") {\n      const next = Math.max(360, Math.min(620, ds.startMid + dx));\n      setMidW(next);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const onPointerUp = () => {\n    dragState.current.dragging = null;\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    };\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [leftW, midW]);\n\n  // Middle list scroll persistence\n  const listScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const listScrollPos = useRef<number>(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) listScrollPos.current = el.scrollTop;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) el.scrollTop = listScrollPos.current;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  // Hide list if focus mode\n  const effectiveListVisible = !focusMode && listVisible;\n\n  // UI text size (+2px)\n  const rootText = \"text-[18px] leading-7\";\n\n  const openProjectDetails = () => {\n    if (!selectedProject || selectedProjectId === inboxId) {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"收件匣不需要專案說明頁\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const openHelp = () => {\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setHelpOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const openSnippets = () => {\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setSnippetsOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const toggleMetaDrawer = () => {\n    // meta is local to editor; keep snippets/project/help mutually exclusive\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen((v) => !v);\n  };\n\n  // When switching prompt, close overlays that might confuse\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    // keep snippets modal state as user controlled\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [selectedPromptId, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // -----------------------\n  // Render\n  // -----------------------\n\n  return (\n    <div className={cx(\"h-screen w-full bg-slate-100\", rootText)}>\n      {/* Top Header */}\n      <div className=\"h-14 shrink-0 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0\">\n          <div className=\"h-9 w-9 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900\" />\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">Prompt Management Workspace</div>\n            <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">專案 × 提示詞 × 編輯（Pin、Drawer、Undo）</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <Button\n            size=\"md\"\n            variant={focusMode ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n            onClick={toggleFocusMode}\n            title=\"專注編輯：隱藏列表，最大化右欄編輯區\"\n            ariaLabel=\"專注編輯\"\n          >\n            {focusMode ? \"退出專注\" : \"專注編輯\"}\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"md\" variant=\"neutral\" onClick={openProjectDetails} title=\"專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期\" ariaLabel=\"專案說明\">\n            專案說明\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"md\" variant=\"neutral\" onClick={openSnippets} title=\"常用片語（右側抽屜）：點擊可插入到游標位置\" ariaLabel=\"片語\">\n            片語\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button\n            size=\"icon\"\n            variant={pinList ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n            ariaLabel=\"釘住提示詞列表\"\n            title={pinList ? \"釘住：列表固定顯示\" : \"未釘住：選取提示詞後自動收合列表\"}\n            onClick={() => {\n              setPinList((v) => !v);\n              if (!pinList) setListVisible(true);\n            }}\n          >\n            📌\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" ariaLabel=\"說明\" title=\"按鈕說明與快捷操作（Help）\" onClick={openHelp}>\n            ?\n          </Button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Body */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 flex overflow-hidden\">\n        {/* Left column */}\n        <aside\n          className={cx(\"shrink-0 border-r bg-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col\", focusMode ? \"w-[72px]\" : \"\")}\n          style={{ width: focusMode ? 72 : leftW }}\n        >\n          <div className={cx(\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">專案</div>\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">（含進度日期）</span>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"font-semibold\">P</div>\n            )}\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <Button\n                size=\"icon\"\n                variant=\"neutral\"\n                title=\"新增專案（原型：未實作）\"\n                ariaLabel=\"新增專案\"\n                onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：新增專案未實作\", undo: null })}\n              >\n                ＋\n              </Button>\n            ) : null}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Inbox */}\n          <div className={cx(\"px-3 py-3 border-b\", focusMode ? \"px-2\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            <button\n              className={cx(\n                \"w-full rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\",\n                selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\"\n              )}\n              onClick={() => onClickProject(inboxId)}\n              title=\"收件匣：未歸檔草稿集中處\"\n              aria-label=\"收件匣\"\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              <div className={cx(\"flex items-center gap-2\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n              >\n                <div className=\"h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center\">📥</div>\n                {!focusMode ? (\n                  <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                    <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">收件匣</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">未歸檔 {inboxCount} 筆</div>\n                  </div>\n                ) : null}\n              </div>\n            </button>\n\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <div className=\"mt-2\">\n                <Button\n                  className=\"w-full\"\n                  size=\"md\"\n                  variant=\"success\"\n                  title=\"新增草稿到收件匣，並直接開啟右欄編輯\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"新增草稿\"\n                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: true })}\n                >\n                  ＋新增草稿\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            ) : null}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Projects list */}\n          <div className={cx(\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2\", focusMode ? \"p-2\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            {projects.map((p) => {\n              const active = selectedProjectId === p.id;\n              return (\n                <div\n                  key={p.id}\n                  className={cx(\"rounded-2xl border bg-white\", active ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\")}\n                >\n                  <button\n                    className={cx(\"w-full p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl transition-colors\")}\n                    onClick={() => onClickProject(p.id)}\n                    title=\"點擊切換專案（若列表已隱藏，點同一專案會叫回列表）\"\n                    aria-label={`專案：${p.name}`}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                  >\n                    <div className={cx(\"flex items-start gap-2\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n                    >\n                      <div className=\"h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center\">📁</div>\n                      {!focusMode ? (\n                        <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                          <div className=\"truncate font-semibold\">{p.name}</div>\n                          <div className=\"mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 min-w-0\">\n                            <span className={cx(\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\", chipTone(p.status))}>{p.status}</span>\n                            <span className=\"truncate\">進度：{p.progressDate || \"—\"}</span>\n                          </div>\n                        </div>\n                      ) : null}\n                      {!focusMode ? (\n                        <Button\n                          size=\"icon\"\n                          variant=\"ghost\"\n                          title=\"專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期\"\n                          ariaLabel=\"專案說明\"\n                          onClick={(e) => {\n                            e.stopPropagation();\n                            setSelectedProjectId(p.id);\n                            openProjectDetails();\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          ℹ️\n                        </Button>\n                      ) : null}\n                    </div>\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n\n        {/* Divider: left */}\n        {!focusMode ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300\"\n            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider(\"left\")}\n            title=\"拖曳調整左欄寬度\"\n          />\n        ) : null}\n\n        {/* Middle column (prompt list) */}\n        {effectiveListVisible ? (\n          <main className=\"shrink-0 border-r bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden flex flex-col\" style={{ width: midW }}>\n            {/* Toolbar */}\n            <div className=\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n              <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">提示詞列表</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">\n                  專案：{selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"收件匣\" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"success\"\n                  title=\"新增提示詞：加入目前專案（或收件匣），並直接開啟右欄編輯\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"新增提示詞\"\n                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: selectedProjectId === inboxId })}\n                >\n                  ＋新增\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  title=\"清除搜尋與篩選條件\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"清除篩選\"\n                  onClick={() => {\n                    setSearch(\"\");\n                    setTypeFilter(\"all\");\n                    setStatusFilter(\"all\");\n                  }}\n                >\n                  清除\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Filters */}\n            <div className=\"px-3 py-3 border-b bg-white\">\n              <input\n                value={search}\n                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n                placeholder=\"搜尋（標題/內容/標籤/備註）\"\n                className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                title=\"搜尋範圍：標題、備註、標籤、正文\"\n              />\n              <div className=\"mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n                <select\n                  value={typeFilter}\n                  onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value)}\n                  className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  title=\"依 type 篩選\"\n                >\n                  {uniqueTypes.map((t) => (\n                    <option key={t} value={t}>\n                      {t === \"all\" ? \"類型：全部\" : t}\n                    </option>\n                  ))}\n                </select>\n                <select\n                  value={statusFilter}\n                  onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}\n                  className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  title=\"依 status 篩選\"\n                >\n                  {uniqueStatuses.map((s) => (\n                    <option key={s} value={s}>\n                      {s === \"all\" ? \"狀態：全部\" : s}\n                    </option>\n                  ))}\n                </select>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* List */}\n            <div ref={listScrollRef} className=\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2\">\n              {filteredPrompts.length === 0 ? <div className=\"text-sm text-slate-500 p-3\">查無符合的提示詞。</div> : null}\n\n              {filteredPrompts.map((p) => {\n                const active = p.id === selectedPromptId;\n                return (\n                  <button\n                    key={p.id}\n                    className={cx(\n                      \"w-full text-left p-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\",\n                      active ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\"\n                    )}\n                    onClick={() => selectPrompt(p.id)}\n                    title=\"點擊開啟右欄編輯（Pin OFF 會自動收合列表）\"\n                    aria-label={`提示詞：${p.fm.title}`}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                  >\n                    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-3\">\n                      <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                        <div className=\"truncate font-semibold\">{p.fm.title}</div>\n                        <div className=\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs\">\n                          <span className={cx(\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\", chipTone(p.fm.status))}>{p.fm.status}</span>\n                          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700\">{p.fm.model}</span>\n                          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700\">{p.fm.type}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div className=\"flex flex-col items-end gap-2\">\n                        <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap\">{formatUpdated(p.fm.updatedAt)}</div>\n                        <Button\n                          size=\"icon\"\n                          variant=\"neutral\"\n                          title=\"更多（原型：未實作清單操作）\"\n                          ariaLabel=\"更多\"\n                          onClick={(e) => {\n                            e.stopPropagation();\n                            setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：列表『更多』未實作\", undo: null });\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          ⋯\n                        </Button>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </button>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </main>\n        ) : (\n          // Small handle to reveal list\n          <button\n            className=\"w-4 shrink-0 bg-indigo-200/70 hover:bg-indigo-300 cursor-pointer relative\"\n            onClick={() => setListVisible(true)}\n            title=\"顯示提示詞列表（小把手）\"\n            aria-label=\"顯示提示詞列表\"\n            type=\"button\"\n          >\n            <div className=\"absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 left-0 w-full text-xs text-indigo-900 select-none\">\n              <div className=\"rotate-90 whitespace-nowrap\">列表</div>\n            </div>\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Divider: middle */}\n        {!focusMode && effectiveListVisible ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300\"\n            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider(\"mid\")}\n            title=\"拖曳調整中欄寬度\"\n          />\n        ) : null}\n\n        {/* Right column (editor) */}\n        <section className=\"flex-1 min-w-[640px] bg-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative\">\n          {/* Sticky header */}\n          <div className=\"sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur border-b\">\n            <div className=\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                <div className=\"truncate font-semibold\">{selectedPrompt ? selectedPrompt.fm.title : \"未選取提示詞\"}</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap\">\n                  {saveState === \"dirty\" ? \"未儲存變更\" : saveState === \"saving\" ? \"儲存中…\" : `已儲存：${selectedPrompt ? formatUpdated(selectedPrompt.fm.updatedAt) : \"—\"}`}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"info\"\n                  onClick={copyToClipboard}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"複製：將右欄正文內容複製到剪貼簿（貼到 ChatGPT/Gemini）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"複製\"\n                >\n                  複製\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  onClick={duplicatePrompt}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"另存為：複製目前提示詞並建立新檔（副本）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"另存為\"\n                >\n                  另存為\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"warn\"\n                  onClick={archivePrompt}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"歸檔：移出目前列表（原型），並自動切換到下一筆\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"歸檔\"\n                >\n                  歸檔\n                </Button>\n                <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"neutral\" onClick={openSnippets} title=\"片語：開啟右側抽屜，點擊插入到游標位置\" ariaLabel=\"片語\">\n                  片語\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant={metaDrawerOpen ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n                  onClick={toggleMetaDrawer}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"Meta：開啟/關閉 Metadata Drawer（YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"Meta\"\n                >\n                  Meta\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"icon\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"更多：顯示更多操作（原型：示範訊息）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"更多\"\n                  onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：更多操作可放移動/刪除/版本歷史等\", undo: null })}\n                >\n                  ⋯\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Quick actions row */}\n            <div className=\"px-3 pb-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"success\"\n                  title=\"新增：建立新提示詞並直接開啟右欄編輯\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"新增\"\n                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: selectedProjectId === inboxId })}\n                >\n                  ＋新增\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  onClick={movePromptMock}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt || selectedProjectId === inboxId}\n                  title=\"移動（示意）：將提示詞移動到另一個專案（原型示意）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"移動\"\n                >\n                  移動\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"danger\"\n                  onClick={deletePrompt}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"刪除：移出目前列表（原型），並自動切換到下一筆（建議正式版進垃圾桶）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"刪除\"\n                >\n                  刪除\n                </Button>\n\n                <div className=\"flex-1\" />\n\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant={prefaceOpen ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n                  disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n                  title=\"前言：每一筆提示詞的前言區（預設收合）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"前言\"\n                  onClick={() => setPrefaceOpen((v) => !v)}\n                >\n                  前言\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Editor area */}\n          <div className={cx(\"flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden\", metaDrawerOpen ? \"pr-[380px]\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            <div className=\"h-full p-3 space-y-3\">\n              {/* Preface (collapsed by default) */}\n              {selectedPrompt && prefaceOpen ? (\n                <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-white shadow-sm overflow-hidden\">\n                  <div className=\"h-11 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n                    <div className=\"font-semibold\">前言（此提示詞專屬）</div>\n                    <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"收起前言\" ariaLabel=\"收起前言\" onClick={() => setPrefaceOpen(false)}>\n                      ✕\n                    </Button>\n                  </div>\n                  <textarea\n                    ref={prefaceRef}\n                    className=\"w-full min-h-[110px] resize-y p-4 outline-none text-[18px] leading-8\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.preface ?? \"\"}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptPreface(e.target.value)}\n                    placeholder=\"在此編輯前言（預設收合；可放背景/上下文/固定規則）\"\n                  />\n                </div>\n              ) : null}\n\n              {/* Main editor */}\n              <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden bg-white shadow-sm\">\n                {!selectedPrompt ? (\n                  <div className=\"h-full grid place-items-center text-slate-500\">請先在列表選取一筆提示詞</div>\n                ) : (\n                  <textarea\n                    ref={editorRef}\n                    className=\"h-full w-full resize-none p-5 outline-none text-[18px] leading-8\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.body}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptBody(e.target.value)}\n                    placeholder=\"在此編輯提示詞正文（未包含 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                    title=\"正文編輯區（建議正式版換成 CodeMirror）\"\n                  />\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Metadata Drawer (inside right pane) */}\n          {metaDrawerOpen && selectedPrompt ? (\n            <div className=\"absolute top-[48px] right-0 bottom-0 w-[380px] border-l bg-white z-20\">\n              <div className=\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">Metadata（frontmatter）</div>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"icon\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  title=\"關閉 Meta\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"關閉 Meta\"\n                  onClick={() => setMetaDrawerOpen(false)}\n                >\n                  ✕\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n\n              <div className=\"p-3 overflow-auto h-[calc(100%-48px)] space-y-3\">\n                <Field label=\"title\">\n                  <input\n                    className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.title}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ title: e.target.value })}\n                    title=\"title：提示詞標題（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"project\">\n                  <input\n                    className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.project}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ project: e.target.value })}\n                    title=\"project：提示詞所屬專案（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n                  <Field label=\"type\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                      value={selectedPrompt.fm.type}\n                      onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ type: e.target.value })}\n                      title=\"type：提示詞類型（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                  <Field label=\"status\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                      value={selectedPrompt.fm.status}\n                      onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ status: e.target.value })}\n                      title=\"status：提示詞狀態（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                </div>\n\n                <Field label=\"model\">\n                  <input\n                    className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.model}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ model: e.target.value })}\n                    title=\"model：使用的模型（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"tags\" hint=\"以逗號分隔\">\n                  <input\n                    className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                    value={(selectedPrompt.fm.tags || []).join(\", \")}\n                    onChange={(e) =>\n                      setPromptFM({\n                        tags: e.target.value\n                          .split(\",\")\n                          .map((s) => s.trim())\n                          .filter(Boolean),\n                      })\n                    }\n                    title=\"tags：標籤（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"note\">\n                  <input\n                    className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.note}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ note: e.target.value })}\n                    title=\"note：備註（對應 YAML frontmatter）\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n                  <Field label=\"createdAt\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                      value={selectedPrompt.fm.createdAt}\n                      readOnly\n                      title=\"createdAt：建立時間（自動）\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                  <Field label=\"updatedAt\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full text-[16px]\"\n                      value={selectedPrompt.fm.updatedAt}\n                      readOnly\n                      title=\"updatedAt：更新時間（自動）\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                </div>\n\n                <div className=\"pt-2\">\n                  <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">正式版可在此編輯 YAML frontmatter，並同步寫入 markdown 檔案。</div>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ) : null}\n\n          {/* Overlay drawers/modals */}\n          <OverlayRightDrawer title=\"常用片語剪貼簿\" open={snippetsOpen} onClose={() => setSnippetsOpen(false)} width={460}>\n            <div className=\"text-sm text-slate-600 mb-3\">點擊片語即可插入至游標位置（不影響左/中欄寬度）。</div>\n            <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n              {DEMO_SNIPPETS.map((s) => (\n                <button\n                  key={s.id}\n                  className=\"w-full text-left rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\"\n                  onClick={() => insertSnippet(s.text)}\n                  title=\"點擊插入到游標位置\"\n                  type=\"button\"\n                >\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                    <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{s.title}</div>\n                    <span className=\"text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700\">{s.tag}</span>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700 whitespace-pre-wrap\">{s.text}</div>\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </OverlayRightDrawer>\n\n          <OverlayModal\n            title={selectedProject ? `專案說明：${selectedProject.name}` : \"專案說明\"}\n            open={projectDetailsOpen}\n            onClose={() => setProjectDetailsOpen(false)}\n            maxWidth={920}\n          >\n            {!selectedProject ? (\n              <div className=\"text-slate-500\">尚未選取專案。</div>\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n                <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-3\">\n                  <Field label=\"進度日期\" hint=\"用於快速定位目前進度\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full\"\n                      type=\"date\"\n                      value={selectedProject.progressDate || \"\"}\n                      onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { progressDate: e.target.value })}\n                      title=\"progressDate：進度日期\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                  <Field label=\"狀態\">\n                    <input\n                      className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border w-full\"\n                      value={selectedProject.status || \"\"}\n                      onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { status: e.target.value })}\n                      title=\"status：專案狀態\"\n                    />\n                  </Field>\n                </div>\n\n                <Field label=\"簡短總結\" hint=\"可貼上本次對話摘要/下一步\">\n                  <textarea\n                    className=\"w-full min-h-[120px] p-3 rounded-2xl border\"\n                    value={selectedProject.summary || \"\"}\n                    onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { summary: e.target.value })}\n                    title=\"summary：專案摘要\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                  <div className=\"font-semibold\">對話/資料連結</div>\n                  <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"success\" title=\"新增一筆連結\" ariaLabel=\"新增連結\" onClick={() => addProjectLink(selectedProject.id)}>\n                    ＋新增連結\n                  </Button>\n                </div>\n\n                <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n                  {(selectedProject.links || []).length === 0 ? title: string;\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  width?: number;\n  zIndex?: number;\n}) {\n  if (!open) return null;\n  return (\n    <div className={cx(\"absolute inset-0\", `z-[${zIndex}]`)} style={{ zIndex }}>\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/10\" onClick={onClose} />\n      <div\n        className=\"absolute right-0 top-0 h-full bg-white shadow-xl border-l\"\n        style={{ width }}\n        role=\"dialog\"\n        aria-modal=\"true\"\n      >\n        <div className=\"h-14 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n          <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{title}</div>\n          <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"關閉\" ariaLabel=\"關閉\" onClick={onClose}>\n            ✕\n          </Button>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"h-[calc(100%-56px)] overflow-auto p-4\">{children}</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction OverlayModal({\n  title,\n  open,\n  onClose,\n  children,\n  maxWidth = 860,\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n  maxWidth?: number;\n}) {\n  if (!open) return null;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed inset-0 z-50\">\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/30\" onClick={onClose} />\n      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 p-6 grid place-items-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-full bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl border overflow-hidden\" style={{ maxWidth }} role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\">\n          <div className=\"h-14 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n            <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{title}</div>\n            <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"關閉\" ariaLabel=\"關閉\" onClick={onClose}>\n              ✕\n            </Button>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"p-4 max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto\">{children}</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Snackbar({ state, onClose }: { state: SnackbarState | null; onClose: () => void }) {\n  if (!state) return null;\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border bg-white shadow-lg px-4 py-3\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-slate-800 max-w-[70vw] truncate\">{state.message}</div>\n        {state.undo ? (\n          <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"復原\" ariaLabel=\"復原\" onClick={() => state.undo?.()}>\n            Undo\n          </Button>\n        ) : null}\n        <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"ghost\" title=\"關閉\" ariaLabel=\"關閉\" onClick={onClose}>\n          ✕\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// -----------------------\n// Main component\n// -----------------------\n\nexport default function App() {\n  // Layout widths (persist)\n  const [leftW, setLeftW] = useLocalStorageState<number>(\"pmw:leftW\", 340);\n  const [midW, setMidW] = useLocalStorageState<number>(\"pmw:midW\", 460);\n\n  // Global behaviors\n  const [pinList, setPinList] = useLocalStorageState<boolean>(\"pmw:pinList\", false);\n\n  // UI state\n  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string>(\"p1\");\n  const [listVisible, setListVisible] = useState<boolean>(true);\n  const [focusMode, setFocusMode] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  const [snippetsOpen, setSnippetsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [metaDrawerOpen, setMetaDrawerOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [projectDetailsOpen, setProjectDetailsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [helpOpen, setHelpOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // Preface UI (per-prompt; default collapsed)\n  const [prefaceOpen, setPrefaceOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // Data state (in-memory)\n  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>(DEMO_PROJECTS);\n  const [promptsByProject, setPromptsByProject] = useState<Record<string, PromptItem[]>>(DEMO_PROMPTS);\n\n  const inboxId = \"__inbox__\";\n  const inboxCount = (promptsByProject[inboxId] ?? []).length;\n\n  const prompts = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n  const [selectedPromptId, setSelectedPromptId] = useState<string | null>(prompts[0]?.id ?? null);\n\n  const selectedProject = useMemo<Project | null>(() => projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId) ?? null, [projects, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Search/filter\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>(\"\");\n  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<string>(\"all\");\n  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>(\"all\");\n\n  // Undo snackbar\n  const [snackbar, setSnackbar] = useState<SnackbarState | null>(null);\n\n  // Editor refs\n  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);\n  const prefaceRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);\n\n  // Keep selection stable when switching project\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    if (!list.length) {\n      setSelectedPromptId(null);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!list.some((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId)) {\n      setSelectedPromptId(list[0].id);\n    }\n  }, [selectedProjectId, promptsByProject, selectedPromptId]);\n\n  const selectedPrompt = useMemo<PromptItem | null>(() => {\n    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    return list.find((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId) ?? null;\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, selectedPromptId]);\n\n  // Expected behavior (confirmed): editing frontmatter.title updates the right-side header immediately.\n  const selectedPromptTitle = selectedPrompt?.fm?.title ?? \"（未選取提示詞）\";\n\n  // Simulated save status\n  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<\"saved\" | \"dirty\" | \"saving\">(\"saved\");\n  const saveTimer = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  const markDirtyAndAutosave = () => {\n    setSaveState(\"dirty\");\n    if (saveTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer.current);\n    saveTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setSaveState(\"saving\");\n      window.setTimeout(() => setSaveState(\"saved\"), 350);\n    }, 700);\n  };\n\n  const setPromptBody = (newBody: string) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) => (p.id === selectedPrompt.id ? { ...p, body: newBody, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } } : p));\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n    markDirtyAndAutosave();\n  };\n\n  const setPromptPreface = (newPreface: string) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) => (p.id === selectedPrompt.id ? { ...p, preface: newPreface, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } } : p));\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n    markDirtyAndAutosave();\n  };\n\n  const setPromptFM = (patch: Partial<Frontmatter>) => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      const next = list.map((p) => (p.id === selectedPrompt.id ? { ...p, fm: { ...p.fm, ...patch, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } } : p));\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };\n    });\n    markDirtyAndAutosave();\n  };\n\n  // Filtering\n  const filteredPrompts = useMemo(() => {\n    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();\n    return (promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [])\n      .filter((p) => {\n        if (typeFilter !== \"all\" && p.fm.type !== typeFilter) return false;\n        if (statusFilter !== \"all\" && p.fm.status !== statusFilter) return false;\n        if (!q) return true;\n        const hay = `${p.fm.title} ${p.fm.note} ${(p.fm.tags || []).join(\" \")} ${p.body}`.toLowerCase();\n        return hay.includes(q);\n      })\n      .slice();\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, search, typeFilter, statusFilter]);\n\n  const uniqueTypes = useMemo(() => {\n    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.type).filter(Boolean));\n    return [\"all\", ...Array.from(set)];\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  const uniqueStatuses = useMemo(() => {\n    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.status).filter(Boolean));\n    return [\"all\", ...Array.from(set)];\n  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Pin OFF behavior: selecting prompt hides list\n  const selectPrompt = (promptId: string) => {\n    setSelectedPromptId(promptId);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n  };\n\n  // Click same project to reveal list (when hidden)\n  const onClickProject = (pid: string) => {\n    if (pid === selectedProjectId) {\n      if (!listVisible) setListVisible(true);\n      return;\n    }\n    setSelectedProjectId(pid);\n    setListVisible(true);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // Focus mode: hide middle list, keep left minimal\n  const toggleFocusMode = () => {\n    setFocusMode((v) => !v);\n    setListVisible(false);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // Helpers for overlays\n  const closeAllOverlays = () => {\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  // CRUD actions\n  const createPrompt = ({ toInbox = false }: { toInbox?: boolean } = {}) => {\n    const id = nextId(toInbox ? \"in\" : \"pr\");\n    const projectName = toInbox ? \"收件匣\" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name;\n\n    const newPrompt: PromptItem = {\n      id,\n      fm: makeFrontmatter({\n        title: \"新草稿\",\n        project: toInbox ? \"收件匣\" : projectName ?? \"\",\n        note: toInbox ? \"inbox\" : \"\",\n      }),\n      preface: \"\",\n      body: \"\",\n    };\n\n    if (toInbox) {\n      setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n        const list = prev[inboxId] ?? [];\n        return { ...prev, [inboxId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n      });\n      setSelectedProjectId(inboxId);\n      setSelectedPromptId(id);\n      setPrefaceOpen(false);\n      setListVisible(pinList ? true : false);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增草稿到收件匣（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增提示詞（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const duplicatePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const id = nextId(\"dup\");\n    const copy: PromptItem = {\n      ...selectedPrompt,\n      id,\n      fm: {\n        ...selectedPrompt.fm,\n        title: `${selectedPrompt.fm.title}（複製）`,\n        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      },\n    };\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [copy, ...list] };\n    });\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已建立副本（另存為）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const archivePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);\n    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已歸檔：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const deletePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);\n    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已刪除：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const movePromptMock = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const target = projects.find((p) => p.id !== selectedProjectId);\n    if (!target) return;\n\n    const fromId = selectedProjectId;\n    const toId = target.id;\n    const moved = selectedPrompt;\n\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[fromId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [toId]: toList, [fromId]: [moved, ...fromList] };\n        });\n        setSelectedProjectId(fromId);\n        setSelectedPromptId(moved.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const copyToClipboard = async () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const text = selectedPrompt.body;\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已複製到剪貼簿\", undo: null });\n    } catch {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"複製失敗：瀏覽器限制\", undo: null });\n    }\n  };\n\n  const insertSnippet = (snippetText: string) => {\n    const el = editorRef.current;\n    if (!el || !selectedPrompt) return;\n    const start = el.selectionStart ?? 0;\n    const end = el.selectionEnd ?? 0;\n    const before = selectedPrompt.body.slice(0, start);\n    const after = selectedPrompt.body.slice(end);\n    const nextText = before + snippetText + after;\n    setPromptBody(nextText);\n\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n      const pos = start + snippetText.length;\n      el.focus();\n      el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);\n    });\n  };\n\n  // Project details (edit fields)\n  const updateProject = (pid: string, patch: Partial<Project>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === pid ? { ...p, ...patch, updatedAt: p.updatedAt } : p)));\n  };\n\n  const addProjectLink = (pid: string) => {\n    const id = nextId(\"link\");\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) =>\n        p.id === pid\n          ? {\n              ...p,\n              links: [...(p.links ?? []), { id, title: \"新連結\", url: \"\" }],\n            }\n          : p\n      )\n    );\n  };\n\n  const updateProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string, patch: Partial<LinkItem>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).map((l) => (l.id === lid ? { ...l, ...patch } : l));\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  const removeProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).filter((l) => l.id !== lid);\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  // Resizer logic\n  const dragState = useRef<{ dragging: null | \"left\" | \"mid\"; startX: number; startLeft: number; startMid: number }>({\n    dragging: null,\n    startX: 0,\n    startLeft: 0,\n    startMid: 0,\n  });\n\n  const onPointerDownDivider = (which: \"left\" | \"mid\") => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    dragState.current.dragging = which;\n    dragState.current.startX = e.clientX;\n    dragState.current.startLeft = leftW;\n    dragState.current.startMid = midW;\n    if (e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture) e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n  };\n\n  const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n    const ds = dragState.current;\n    if (!ds.dragging) return;\n    const dx = e.clientX - ds.startX;\n\n    if (ds.dragging === \"left\") {\n      const next = Math.max(280, Math.min(460, ds.startLeft + dx));\n      setLeftW(next);\n    }\n\n    if (ds.dragging === \"mid\") {\n      const next = Math.max(360, Math.min(620, ds.startMid + dx));\n      setMidW(next);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const onPointerUp = () => {\n    dragState.current.dragging = null;\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    };\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [leftW, midW]);\n\n  // Middle list scroll persistence\n  const listScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const listScrollPos = useRef<number>(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) listScrollPos.current = el.scrollTop;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) el.scrollTop = listScrollPos.current;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  // Hide list if focus mode\n  const effectiveListVisible = !focusMode && listVisible;\n\n  // UI text size (+2px)\n  const rootText = \"text-[18px] leading-7\";\n\n  const openProjectDetails = () => {\n    if (!selectedProject || selectedProjectId === inboxId) {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"收件匣不需要專案說明頁\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const openHelp = () => {\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setHelpOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const toggleSnippets = () => {\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen((v) => !v);\n  };\n\n  const toggleMetaDrawer = () => {\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen((v) => !v);\n  };\n\n  // When switching prompt, close drawers that might confuse\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [selectedPromptId, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Self tests (lightweight)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      const id1 = nextId(\"t\");\n      const id2 = nextId(\"t\");\n      console.assert(id1 !== id2, \"nextId should generate different values\");\n      console.assert(chipTone(\"使用中\").includes(\"emerald\"), \"chipTone should map 使用中 to emerald\");\n      console.assert(formatUpdated(new Date().toISOString()).includes(\"/\"), \"formatUpdated should produce MM/DD\");\n    } catch {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const saveBadge = useMemo(() => {\n    if (saveState === \"saved\") return { text: \"已儲存\", cls: \"bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200\" };\n    if (saveState === \"saving\") return { text: \"儲存中…\", cls: \"bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border-sky-200\" };\n    return { text: \"未儲存\", cls: \"bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200\" };\n  }, [saveState]);\n\n  const wordCount = useMemo(() => {\n    const s = selectedPrompt?.body ?? \"\";\n    const trimmed = s.trim();\n    if (!trimmed) return 0;\n    return trimmed.split(/\\s+/g).length;\n  }, [selectedPrompt?.body]);\n\n  // -----------------------\n  // Render\n  // -----------------------\n\n  return (\n    <div className={cx(\"h-screen w-full bg-slate-100\", rootText)}>\n      {/* Top Header */}\n      <div className=\"h-14 shrink-0 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0\">\n          <div className=\"h-9 w-9 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900\" />\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">Prompt Management Workspace</div>\n            <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">專案 × 提示詞 × 編輯（Pin、Drawer、Undo）</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <Button\n            size=\"md\"\n            variant={focusMode ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n            onClick={toggleFocusMode}\n            title=\"專注編輯：隱藏列表，最大化右欄編輯區\"\n            ariaLabel=\"專注編輯\"\n          >\n            {focusMode ? \"退出專注\" : \"專注編輯\"}\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"md\" variant=\"neutral\" onClick={openProjectDetails} title=\"專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期\" ariaLabel=\"專案說明\">\n            專案說明\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"md\" variant=\"neutral\" onClick={toggleSnippets} title=\"常用片語（右欄抽屜）：點擊可插入到游標位置\" ariaLabel=\"片語\">\n            片語\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button\n            size=\"icon\"\n            variant={pinList ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"}\n            ariaLabel=\"釘住提示詞列表\"\n            title={pinList ? \"釘住：列表固定顯示\" : \"未釘住：選取提示詞後自動收合列表\"}\n            onClick={() => {\n              setPinList((v) => !v);\n              if (!pinList) setListVisible(true);\n            }}\n          >\n            📌\n          </Button>\n\n          <Button size=\"icon\" variant=\"neutral\" ariaLabel=\"說明\" title=\"按鈕說明與快捷操作（Help）\" onClick={openHelp}>\n            ?\n          </Button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Body */}\n      <div
