\"w-[72px]\" : \"\")}\n          style={{ width: focusMode ? 72 : leftW }}\n        >\n          <div className={cx(\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">專案</div>\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">（含進度日期）</span>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"font-semibold\">P</div>\n            )}\n\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <Button\n                size=\"icon\"\n                variant=\"neutral\"\n                title=\"新增專案（原型：未實作）\"\n                ariaLabel=\"新增專案\"\n                onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：新增專案未實作\", undo: null })}\n              >\n                ＋\n              </Button>\n            ) : null}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Inbox */}\n          <div className={cx(\"px-3 py-3 border-b\", focusMode ? \"px-2\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            <button\n              className={cx(\n                \"w-full rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\",\n                selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\"\n              )}\n              onClick={() => onClickProject(inboxId)}\n              title=\"收件匣：未歸檔草稿集中處\"\n              aria-label=\"收件匣\"\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              <div className={cx(\"flex items-center gap-2\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n              >\n                <div className=\"h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center\">📥</div>\n                {!focusMode ? (\n                  <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                    <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">收件匣</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">未歸檔 {inboxCount} 筆</div>\n                  </div>\n                ) : null}\n              </div>\n            </button>\n\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <div className=\"mt-2\">\n                <Button\n                  className=\"w-full\"\n                  size=\"md\"\n                  variant=\"success\"\n                  title=\"新增草稿到收件匣，並直接開啟右欄編輯\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"新增草稿\"\n                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: true })}\n                >\n                  ＋新增草稿\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            ) : null}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Projects list */}\n          <div className={cx(\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2\", focusMode ? \"p-2\" : \"\")}\n          >\n            {projects.map((p) => {\n              const active = selectedProjectId === p.id;\n              return (\n                <div key={p.id} className={cx(\"rounded-2xl border bg-white\", active ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\")}>\n                  <button\n                    className=\"w-full p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl transition-colors\"\n                    onClick={() => onClickProject(p.id)}\n                    title=\"點擊切換專案（若列表已隱藏，點同一專案會叫回列表）\"\n                    aria-label={`專案：${p.name}`}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                  >\n                    <div className={cx(\"flex items-start gap-2\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}\n                    >\n                      <div className=\"h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center\">📁</div>\n                      {!focusMode ? (\n                        <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                          <div className=\"truncate font-semibold\">{p.name}</div>\n                          <div className=\"mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 min-w-0\">\n                            <span className={cx(\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\", chipTone(p.status))}>{p.status}</span>\n                            <span className=\"truncate\">進度：{p.progressDate || \"—\"}</span>\n                          </div>\n                        </div>\n                      ) : null}\n\n                      {!focusMode ? (\n                        <Button\n                          size=\"icon\"\n                          variant=\"ghost\"\n                          title=\"專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期\"\n                          ariaLabel=\"專案說明\"\n                          onClick={(e) => {\n                            e.stopPropagation();\n                            setSelectedProjectId(p.id);\n                            openProjectDetails();\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          ℹ️\n                        </Button>\n                      ) : null}\n                    </div>\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n\n        {/* Divider: left */}\n        {!focusMode ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300\"\n            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider(\"left\")}\n            title=\"拖曳調整左欄寬度\"\n          />\n        ) : null}\n\n        {/* Middle column (prompt list) */}\n        {effectiveListVisible ? (\n          <main className=\"shrink-0 border-r bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden flex flex-col\" style={{ width: midW }}>\n            {/* Toolbar */}\n            <div className=\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n              <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">提示詞列表</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">\n                  專案：{selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"收件匣\" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"success\"\n                  title=\"新增提示詞：加入目前專案（或收件匣），並直接開啟右欄編輯\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"新增提示詞\"\n                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: selectedProjectId === inboxId })}\n                >\n                  ＋新增\n                </Button>\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  title=\"清除搜尋與篩選條件\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"清除篩選\"\n                  onClick={() => {\n                    setSearch(\"\");\n                    setTypeFilter(\"all\");\n                    setStatusFilter(\"all\");\n                  }}\n                >\n                  清除\n                </Button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Filters */}\n            <div className=\"px-3 py-3 border-b bg-white\">\n              <input\n                value={search}\n                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n                placeholder=\"搜尋（標題/內容/標籤/備註）\"\n                className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                title=\"搜尋範圍：標題、備註、標籤、正文\"\n              />\n              <div className=\"mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n                <select value={typeFilter} onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value)} className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\" title=\"依 type 篩選\">\n                  {uniqueTypes.map((t) => (\n                    <option key={t} value={t}>\n                      {t === \"all\" ? \"類型：全部\" : t}\n                    </option>\n                  ))}\n                </select>\n                <select value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} className=\"h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\" title=\"依 status 篩選\">\n                  {uniqueStatuses.map((s) => (\n                    <option key={s} value={s}>\n                      {s === \"all\" ? \"狀態：全部\" : s}\n                    </option>\n                  ))}\n                </select>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            {/* List */}\n            <div ref={listScrollRef} className=\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2\">\n              {filteredPrompts.length === 0 ? <div className=\"text-sm text-slate-500 p-3\">查無符合的提示詞。</div> : null}\n\n              {filteredPrompts.map((p) => {\n                const active = p.id === selectedPromptId;\n                return (\n                  <button\n                    key={p.id}\n                    className={cx(\n                      \"w-full text-left p-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\",\n                      active ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\"\n                    )}\n                    onClick={() => selectPrompt(p.id)}\n                    title=\"點擊開啟右欄編輯（Pin OFF 會自動收合列表）\"\n                    aria-label={`提示詞：${p.fm.title}`}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                  >\n                    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-3\">\n                      <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                        <div className=\"truncate font-semibold\">{p.fm.title}</div>\n                        <div className=\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs\">\n                          <span className={cx(\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\", chipTone(p.fm.status))}>{p.fm.status}</span>\n                          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700\">{p.fm.model}</span>\n                          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700\">{p.fm.type}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div className=\"flex flex-col items-end gap-2\">\n                        <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap\">{formatUpdated(p.fm.updatedAt)}</div>\n                        <Button\n                          size=\"icon\"\n                          variant=\"neutral\"\n                          title=\"更多（原型：未實作清單操作）\"\n                          ariaLabel=\"更多\"\n                          onClick={(e) => {\n                            e.stopPropagation();\n                            setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：列表『更多』未實作\", undo: null });\n                          }}\n                        >\n                          ⋯\n                        </Button>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </button>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </main>\n        ) : (\n          // Small handle to reveal list\n          <button\n            className=\"w-4 shrink-0 bg-indigo-200/70 hover:bg-indigo-300 cursor-pointer relative\"\n            onClick={() => setListVisible(true)}\n            title=\"顯示提示詞列表（小把手）\"\n            aria-label=\"顯示提示詞列表\"\n            type=\"button\"\n          >\n            <div className=\"absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 left-0 w-full text-xs text-indigo-900 select-none\">\n              <div className=\"rotate-90 whitespace-nowrap\">列表</div>\n            </div>\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Divider: middle */}\n        {!focusMode && effectiveListVisible ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300\"\n            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider(\"mid\")}\n            title=\"拖曳調整中欄寬度\"\n          />\n        ) : null}\n\n        {/* Right column (editor) */}\n        <section className=\"flex-1 min-w-0 bg-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative\">\n          {/* Editor top bar */}\n          <div className=\"h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n            <div className=\"min-w-0 flex items-center gap-3\">\n              {!effectiveListVisible ? (\n                <Button\n                  size=\"sm\"\n                  variant=\"neutral\"\n                  title=\"叫回提示詞列表（等同點左欄同專案一次）\"\n                  ariaLabel=\"叫回列表\"\n                  onClick={() => setListVisible(true)}\n                >\n                  叫回列表\n                </Button>\n              ) : null}\n\n              <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\" title={selectedPromptTitle}>\n                  {selectedPromptTitle}\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">\n                  {selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"收件匣\" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name}\n                  {selectedPrompt ? ` · ${wordCount} words` : \"\"}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n\n              <span className={cx(\"px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-xs\", saveBadge.cls)} title=\"自動儲存狀態（原型）\">\n                {saveBadge.text}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"前言：每筆提示詞獨立前言（預設收合）\" ariaLabel=\"前言\" onClick={() => setPrefaceOpen((v) => !v)} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                {prefaceOpen ? \"收起前言\" : \"展開前言\"}\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant={metaDrawerOpen ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"} title=\"Metadata Drawer：title/type/status/tags…（右欄滑出）\" ariaLabel=\"Metadata\" onClick={toggleMetaDrawer} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                Metadata\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant={snippetsOpen ? \"primary\" : \"neutral\"} title=\"片語 Drawer：點擊插入游標位置（右欄滑出）\" ariaLabel=\"片語\" onClick={toggleSnippets} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                片語\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"info\" title=\"複製正文到剪貼簿\" ariaLabel=\"複製\" onClick={copyToClipboard} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                複製\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"建立副本（另存為）\" ariaLabel=\"複製副本\" onClick={duplicatePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                副本\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"neutral\" title=\"移動到另一專案（示意）\" ariaLabel=\"移動\" onClick={movePromptMock} disabled={!selectedPrompt || selectedProjectId === inboxId}>\n                移動\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"warn\" title=\"歸檔：移除目前提示詞（可 Undo；會自動開下一筆）\" ariaLabel=\"歸檔\" onClick={archivePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                歸檔\n              </Button>\n\n              <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"danger\" title=\"刪除：移除目前提示詞（可 Undo；會自動開下一筆）\" ariaLabel=\"刪除\" onClick={deletePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                刪除\n              </Button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Preface (collapsible) */}\n          {prefaceOpen ? (\n            <div className=\"px-3 py-3 border-b bg-slate-50\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">前言（每筆提示詞獨立）</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">預設收合；展開後可編輯</div>\n              </div>\n              <textarea\n                ref={prefaceRef}\n                value={selectedPrompt?.preface ?? \"\"}\n                onChange={(e) => setPromptPreface(e.target.value)}\n                className=\"mt-2 w-full h-28 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3 text-[16px] leading-6\"\n                placeholder=\"可放固定背景/上下文（例如專案共通描述、角色設定）。\"\n                disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n              />\n            </div>\n          ) : null}\n\n          {/* Editor */}\n          <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 p-3\">\n            <div className=\"h-full rounded-3xl border bg-white overflow-hidden shadow-sm\">\n              <div className=\"h-10 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50\">\n                <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold\">目前編輯中（單欄純編輯）</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">建議外接螢幕：1920×1440</div>\n              </div>\n              <textarea\n                ref={editorRef}\n                value={selectedPrompt?.body ?? \"\"}\n                onChange={(e) => setPromptBody(e.target.value)}\n                className=\"w-full h-[calc(100%-40px)] p-4 text-[16px] leading-7 font-mono outline-none\"\n                placeholder={selectedPrompt ? \"在這裡輸入提示詞內容…\" : \"請先在左/中欄選取提示詞或新增。\"}\n                disabled={!selectedPrompt}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Metadata Drawer (inside right pane) */}\n          <PaneRightDrawer title=\"Metadata\" open={metaDrawerOpen} onClose={() => setMetaDrawerOpen(false)} width={460} zIndex={40}>\n            {!selectedPrompt ? <div className=\"text-sm text-slate-500\">尚未選取提示詞。</div> : (\n              <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n                <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700\">\n                  <div className=\"font-semibold\">按鈕作用</div>\n                  <ul className=\"mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1\">\n                    <li>此 Drawer 用於編輯 frontmatter（title/type/status/model/tags…）。</li>\n                    <li>你已確認：修改 title 需即時反映到右欄標題。</li>\n                  </ul>\n                </div>\n\n                <Field label=\"title\" hint=\"右欄標題即時更新\">\n                  <input\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.title}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ title: e.target.value })}\n                    className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"project\" hint=\"顯示用，不做跨專案同步\">\n                  <input value={selectedPrompt.fm.project} readOnly className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 text-[16px]\" />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"type\">\n                  <select\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.type}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ type: e.target.value })}\n                    className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  >\n                    {Array.from(new Set([\"其他\", \"模板\", \"需求\", \"UIUX\", \"技術\", ...(uniqueTypes.filter((t) => t !== \"all\") as string[])]))\n                      .filter(Boolean)\n                      .map((t) => (\n                        <option key={t} value={t}>\n                          {t}\n                        </option>\n                      ))}\n                  </select>\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"status\">\n                  <select\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.status}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ status: e.target.value })}\n                    className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  >\n                    {Array.from(new Set([\"使用中\", \"進行中\", \"封存\", ...(uniqueStatuses.filter((s) => s !== \"all\") as string[])]))\n                      .filter(Boolean)\n                      .map((s) => (\n                        <option key={s} value={s}>\n                          {s}\n                        </option>\n                      ))}\n                  </select>\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"model\">\n                  <input\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.model}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ model: e.target.value })}\n                    className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                    placeholder=\"例如 gpt-4o-mini\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"tags\" hint=\"以逗號分隔\">\n                  <input\n                    value={(selectedPrompt.fm.tags ?? []).join(\", \")}\n                    onChange={(e) => {\n                      const tags = e.target.value\n                        .split(\",\")\n                        .map((x) => x.trim())\n                    placeholder=\"例如 UIUX, 模板, SA\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <Field label=\"note\">\n                  <input\n                    value={selectedPrompt.fm.note}\n                    onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ note: e.target.value })}\n                    className=\"w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]\"\n                  />\n                </Field>\n\n                <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n                  <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3\">\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">createdAt</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold break-all\">{selectedPrompt.fm.createdAt}</div>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3\">\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">updatedAt</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold break-all\">{selectedPrompt.fm.updatedAt}</div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n\n                <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900\">\n                  提示：你的 Markdown frontmatter 會是 YAML，例如：<br />\n                  <code className=\"text-xs\">title / project / type / status / model / tags / note / updatedAt / createdAt</code>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </PaneRightDrawer>\n\n          {/* Snippets Drawer (inside right pane) */}\n          <PaneRightDrawer title=\"常用片語剪貼簿\" open={snippetsOpen} onClose={() => setSnippetsOpen(false)} width={420} zIndex={30}>\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              <div className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">使用方式</div>\n                <ul className=\"mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1\">\n                  <li>點擊片語 → 插入到正文游標位置</li>\n                  <li>不影響左/中欄寬度（右欄內滑出）</li>\n                </ul>\n              </div>\n\n              {DEMO_SNIPPETS.map((s) => (\n                <div key={s.id} className=\"rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n                    <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n                      <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">{s.title}</div>\n                      <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">{s.tag}</div>\n                    </div>\n                    <Button size=\"sm\" variant=\"primary\" title=\"插入到游標位置\" ariaLabel=\"插入\" onClick={() => insertSnippet(s.text)} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>\n                      插入\n                    </Button>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words\">{s.text}</div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n\n              <Button\n                className=\"w-full\"\n                size=\"md\"\n                variant=\"neutral\"\n                title=\"新增片語（原型：未實作）\"\n                ariaLabel=\"新增片語\"\n                onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：新增片語未實作\", undo: null })}\n              >\n                ＋新增片語（原型）\n              </Button>\n            </div>\n          </PaneRightDrawer>\n        </section>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Modals */}\n      <OverlayModal title=\"專案說明\" open={projectDetailsOpen} onClose={() => setProjectDetailsOpen(false)}>\n        {!selectedProject ? 新的版本已確保：

- 所有 JSX 標籤 **完整閉合**
- 右欄的兩個 Drawer（**Metadata**、**片語**）都在 **右欄內滑出**，不影響左/中欄
- Divider 與條件渲染結構已整理，避免少一個 `</div>` 之類的錯
- 額外補上 **最基本的 self-tests**（`console.assert`），符合「沒有測試就要補測試」的要求

1) **「歸檔」**在你的預期中，是

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

import type { Frontmatter, LinkItem, Project, PromptItem, SnackbarState } from "./types";
import { DEMO_PROJECTS, DEMO_PROMPTS, DEMO_SNIPPETS, makeFrontmatter } from "./data/demo";
import { chipTone, cx, formatUpdated, nextId, useLocalStorageState } from "./utils/helpers";

import { Button } from "./components/ui/Button";
import { Field } from "./components/ui/Field";
import { OverlayModal } from "./components/ui/OverlayModal";
import { PaneRightDrawer } from "./components/ui/PaneRightDrawer";
import { Snackbar } from "./components/ui/Snackbar";
import { runSelfTests } from "./selfTests";

/**
 * Prompt Management Workspace (UI Prototype)
 * - Tailwind CSS classes
 * - No backend; in-memory demo data
 * - Split into multiple files for easier maintenance.
 */

export default function App() {
  // Layout widths (persist)
  const [leftW, setLeftW] = useLocalStorageState<number>("pmw:leftW", 340);
  const [midW, setMidW] = useLocalStorageState<number>("pmw:midW", 460);

  // Global behaviors
  const [pinList, setPinList] = useLocalStorageState<boolean>("pmw:pinList", false);

  // UI state
  const [selectedProjectId, setSelectedProjectId] = useState<string>("p1");
  const [listVisible, setListVisible] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [focusMode, setFocusMode] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [snippetsOpen, setSnippetsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [metaDrawerOpen, setMetaDrawerOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [projectDetailsOpen, setProjectDetailsOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [helpOpen, setHelpOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Preface UI (per-prompt; default collapsed)
  const [prefaceOpen, setPrefaceOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Data state (in-memory)
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>(DEMO_PROJECTS);
  const [promptsByProject, setPromptsByProject] = useState<Record<string, PromptItem[]>>(DEMO_PROMPTS);

  const inboxId = "__inbox__";
  const inboxCount = (promptsByProject[inboxId] ?? []).length;

  const [selectedPromptId, setSelectedPromptId] = useState<string | null>((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [])[0]?.id ?? null);

  const selectedProject = useMemo<Project | null>(
    () => projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId) ?? null,
    [projects, selectedProjectId]
  );

  // Search/filter
  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>("");
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>("all");

  // Undo snackbar
  const [snackbar, setSnackbar] = useState<SnackbarState | null>(null);

  // Editor refs
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);
  const prefaceRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null);

  // Keep selection stable when switching project
  useEffect(() => {
    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
    if (!list.length) {
      setSelectedPromptId(null);
      return;
    }
    if (!list.some((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId)) {
      setSelectedPromptId(list[0].id);
    }
  }, [selectedProjectId, promptsByProject, selectedPromptId]);

  const selectedPrompt = useMemo<PromptItem | null>(() => {
    const list = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
    return list.find((p) => p.id === selectedPromptId) ?? null;
  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, selectedPromptId]);

  // Confirmed behavior: editing frontmatter.title updates the right-side header immediately.
  const selectedPromptTitle = selectedPrompt?.fm?.title ?? "（未選取提示詞）";

  // Simulated save status
  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<"saved" | "dirty" | "saving">("saved");
  const saveTimer = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const markDirtyAndAutosave = () => {
    setSaveState("dirty");
    if (saveTimer.current) window.clearTimeout(saveTimer.current);
    saveTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setSaveState("saving");
      window.setTimeout(() => setSaveState("saved"), 350);
    }, 700);
  };

  const setPromptBody = (newBody: string) => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      const next = list.map((p) =>
        p.id === selectedPrompt.id
          ? { ...p, body: newBody, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }
          : p
      );
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };
    });
    markDirtyAndAutosave();
  };

  const setPromptPreface = (newPreface: string) => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      const next = list.map((p) =>
        p.id === selectedPrompt.id
          ? { ...p, preface: newPreface, fm: { ...p.fm, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }
          : p
      );
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };
    });
    markDirtyAndAutosave();
  };

  const setPromptFM = (patch: Partial<Frontmatter>) => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      const next = list.map((p) =>
        p.id === selectedPrompt.id
          ? { ...p, fm: { ...p.fm, ...patch, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } }
          : p
      );
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: next };
    });
    markDirtyAndAutosave();
  };

  // Filtering
  const filteredPrompts = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    return (promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [])
      .filter((p) => {
        if (typeFilter !== "all" && p.fm.type !== typeFilter) return false;
        if (statusFilter !== "all" && p.fm.status !== statusFilter) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        const hay = `${p.fm.title} ${p.fm.note} ${(p.fm.tags || []).join(" ")} ${p.body}`.toLowerCase();
        return hay.includes(q);
      })
      .slice();
  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId, search, typeFilter, statusFilter]);

  const uniqueTypes = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.type).filter(Boolean));
    return ["all", ...Array.from(set)];
  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);

  const uniqueStatuses = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set((promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? []).map((p) => p.fm.status).filter(Boolean));
    return ["all", ...Array.from(set)];
  }, [promptsByProject, selectedProjectId]);

  // Pin OFF behavior: selecting prompt hides list
  const selectPrompt = (promptId: string) => {
    setSelectedPromptId(promptId);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);
  };

  // Click same project to reveal list (when hidden)
  const onClickProject = (pid: string) => {
    if (pid === selectedProjectId) {
      if (!listVisible) setListVisible(true);
      return;
    }
    setSelectedProjectId(pid);
    setListVisible(true);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
  };

  // Focus mode: hide middle list
  const toggleFocusMode = () => {
    setFocusMode((v) => !v);
    setListVisible(false);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
  };

  // Helpers for overlays
  const closeAllOverlays = () => {
    setSnippetsOpen(false);
    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);
    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);
    setHelpOpen(false);
  };

  // CRUD actions
  const createPrompt = ({ toInbox = false }: { toInbox?: boolean } = {}) => {
    const id = nextId(toInbox ? "in" : "pr");
    const projectName = toInbox ? "收件匣" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name;

    const newPrompt: PromptItem = {
      id,
      fm: makeFrontmatter({
        title: "新草稿",
        project: toInbox ? "收件匣" : projectName ?? "",
        note: toInbox ? "inbox" : "",
      }),
      preface: "",
      body: "",
    };

    if (toInbox) {
      setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
        const list = prev[inboxId] ?? [];
        return { ...prev, [inboxId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };
      });
      setSelectedProjectId(inboxId);
      setSelectedPromptId(id);
      setPrefaceOpen(false);
      setListVisible(pinList ? true : false);
      setSnackbar({ message: "已新增草稿到收件匣（已開啟編輯）", undo: null });
      return;
    }

    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };
    });

    setSelectedPromptId(id);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);
    setSnackbar({ message: "已新增提示詞（已開啟編輯）", undo: null });
  };

  const duplicatePrompt = () => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    const id = nextId("dup");
    const copy: PromptItem = {
      ...selectedPrompt,
      id,
      fm: {
        ...selectedPrompt.fm,
        title: `${selectedPrompt.fm.title}（複製）`,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };

    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [copy, ...list] };
    });
    setSelectedPromptId(id);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);

    setSnackbar({ message: "已建立副本（另存為）", undo: null });
  };

  const archivePrompt = () => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    const removed = selectedPrompt;

    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);
    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;

    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };
    });

    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);

    setSnackbar({
      message: `已歸檔：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,
      undo: () => {
        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };
        });
        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);
      },
    });
  };

  const deletePrompt = () => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    const removed = selectedPrompt;

    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removed.id);
    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;

    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };
    });

    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);

    setSnackbar({
      message: `已刪除：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,
      undo: () => {
        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];
          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };
        });
        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);
      },
    });
  };

  const movePromptMock = () => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    const target = projects.find((p) => p.id !== selectedProjectId);
    if (!target) return;

    const fromId = selectedProjectId;
    const toId = target.id;
    const moved = selectedPrompt;

    const currentList = promptsByProject[fromId] ?? [];
    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === moved.id);
    const next = currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;

    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
      const fromList = (prev[fromId] ?? []).filter((p) => p.id !== moved.id);
      const toList = prev[toId] ?? [];
      const moved2: PromptItem = { ...moved, fm: { ...moved.fm, project: target.name, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() } };
      return { ...prev, [fromId]: fromList, [toId]: [moved2, ...toList] };
    });

    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);

    setSnackbar({
      message: `已移動到：${target.name}（示意）`,
      undo: () => {
        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {
          const toList = (prev[toId] ?? []).filter((p) => p.id !== moved.id);
          const fromList = prev[fromId] ?? [];
          return { ...prev, [toId]: toList, [fromId]: [moved, ...fromList] };
        });
        setSelectedProjectId(fromId);
        setSelectedPromptId(moved.id);
      },
    });
  };

  const copyToClipboard = async () => {
    if (!selectedPrompt) return;
    const text = selectedPrompt.body;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setSnackbar({ message: "已複製到剪貼簿", undo: null });
    } catch {
      setSnackbar({ message: "複製失敗：瀏覽器限制", undo: null });
    }
  };

  const insertSnippet = (snippetText: string) => {
    const el = editorRef.current;
    if (!el || !selectedPrompt) return;
    const start = el.selectionStart ?? 0;
    const end = el.selectionEnd ?? 0;
    const before = selectedPrompt.body.slice(0, start);
    const after = selectedPrompt.body.slice(end);
    const nextText = before + snippetText + after;
    setPromptBody(nextText);

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const pos = start + snippetText.length;
      el.focus();
      el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);
    });
  };

  // Project details (edit fields)
  const updateProject = (pid: string, patch: Partial<Project>) => {
    setProjects((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === pid ? { ...p, ...patch, updatedAt: p.updatedAt } : p)));
  };

  const addProjectLink = (pid: string) => {
    const id = nextId("link");
    setProjects((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.id === pid ? { ...p, links: [...(p.links ?? []), { id, title: "新連結", url: "" }] } : p))
    );
  };

  const updateProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string, patch: Partial<LinkItem>) => {
    setProjects((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => {
        if (p.id !== pid) return p;
        const links = (p.links ?? []).map((l) => (l.id === lid ? { ...l, ...patch } : l));
        return { ...p, links };
      })
    );
  };

  const removeProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string) => {
    setProjects((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => {
        if (p.id !== pid) return p;
        const links = (p.links ?? []).filter((l) => l.id !== lid);
        return { ...p, links };
      })
    );
  };

  // Resizer logic
  const dragState = useRef<{ dragging: null | "left" | "mid"; startX: number; startLeft: number; startMid: number }>(
    { dragging: null, startX: 0, startLeft: 0, startMid: 0 }
  );

  const onPointerDownDivider = (which: "left" | "mid") => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    dragState.current.dragging = which;
    dragState.current.startX = e.clientX;
    dragState.current.startLeft = leftW;
    dragState.current.startMid = midW;
    if (e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture) e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  };

  const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    const ds = dragState.current;
    if (!ds.dragging) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - ds.startX;

    if (ds.dragging === "left") {
      const next = Math.max(280, Math.min(460, ds.startLeft + dx));
      setLeftW(next);
    }

    if (ds.dragging === "mid") {
      const next = Math.max(360, Math.min(620, ds.startMid + dx));
      setMidW(next);
    }
  };

  const onPointerUp = () => {
    dragState.current.dragging = null;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", onPointerMove);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", onPointerUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", onPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", onPointerUp);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [leftW, midW]);

  // Middle list scroll persistence
  const listScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const listScrollPos = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listVisible) {
      const el = listScrollRef.current;
      if (el) listScrollPos.current = el.scrollTop;
    }
  }, [listVisible]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (listVisible) {
      const el = listScrollRef.current;
      if (el) el.scrollTop = listScrollPos.current;
    }
  }, [listVisible]);

  // Hide list if focus mode
  const effectiveListVisible = !focusMode && listVisible;

  // UI text size (+2px)
  const rootText = "text-[18px] leading-7";

  const openProjectDetails = () => {
    if (!selectedProject || selectedProjectId === inboxId) {
      setSnackbar({ message: "收件匣不需要專案說明頁", undo: null });
      return;
    }
    closeAllOverlays();
    setProjectDetailsOpen(true);
  };

  const openHelp = () => {
    closeAllOverlays();
    setHelpOpen(true);
  };

  const toggleSnippets = () => {
    setHelpOpen(false);
    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);
    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);
    setSnippetsOpen((v) => !v);
  };

  const toggleMetaDrawer = () => {
    setHelpOpen(false);
    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);
    setSnippetsOpen(false);
    setMetaDrawerOpen((v) => !v);
  };

  // When switching prompt/project, close drawers
  useEffect(() => {
    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);
    setSnippetsOpen(false);
    setPrefaceOpen(false);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [selectedPromptId, selectedProjectId]);

  // Self-tests (lightweight)
  useEffect(() => {
    runSelfTests();
  }, []);

  const saveBadge = useMemo(() => {
    if (saveState === "saved") return { text: "已儲存", cls: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200" };
    if (saveState === "saving") return { text: "儲存中…", cls: "bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border-sky-200" };
    return { text: "未儲存", cls: "bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200" };
  }, [saveState]);

  const wordCount = useMemo(() => {
    const s = selectedPrompt?.body ?? "";
    const trimmed = s.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return 0;
    return trimmed.split(/\s+/g).length;
  }, [selectedPrompt?.body]);

  return (
    <div className={cx("h-screen w-full bg-slate-100", rootText)}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <div className="h-14 shrink-0 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
          <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900" />
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="font-semibold truncate">Prompt Management Workspace</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">專案 × 提示詞 × 編輯（Pin、Drawer、Undo）</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button
            size="md"
            variant={focusMode ? "primary" : "neutral"}
            onClick={toggleFocusMode}
            title="專注編輯：隱藏列表，最大化右欄編輯區"
            ariaLabel="專注編輯"
          >
            {focusMode ? "退出專注" : "專注編輯"}
          </Button>

          <Button size="md" variant="neutral" onClick={openProjectDetails} title="專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期" ariaLabel="專案說明">
            專案說明
          </Button>

          <Button size="md" variant="neutral" onClick={toggleSnippets} title="常用片語（右欄抽屜）：點擊可插入到游標位置" ariaLabel="片語">
            片語
          </Button>

          <Button
            size="icon"
            variant={pinList ? "primary" : "neutral"}
            ariaLabel="釘住提示詞列表"
            title={pinList ? "釘住：列表固定顯示" : "未釘住：選取提示詞後自動收合列表"}
            onClick={() => {
              setPinList((v) => !v);
              if (!pinList) setListVisible(true);
            }}
          >
            📌
          </Button>

          <Button size="icon" variant="neutral" ariaLabel="說明" title="按鈕說明與快捷操作（Help）" onClick={openHelp}>
            ?
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Body */}
      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex overflow-hidden">
        {/* Left column */}
        <aside
          className={cx("shrink-0 border-r bg-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col", focusMode ? "w-[72px]" : "")}
          style={{ width: focusMode ? 72 : leftW }}
        >
          <div className={cx("h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b", focusMode ? "justify-center" : "")}
          >
            {!focusMode ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="font-semibold">專案</div>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">（含進度日期）</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="font-semibold">P</div>
            )}

            {!focusMode ? (
              <Button
                size="icon"
                variant="neutral"
                title="新增專案（原型：未實作）"
                ariaLabel="新增專案"
                onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: "原型：新增專案未實作", undo: null })}
              >
                ＋
              </Button>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          {/* Inbox */}
          <div className={cx("px-3 py-3 border-b", focusMode ? "px-2" : "")}
          >
            <button
              className={cx(
                "w-full rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors",
                selectedProjectId === inboxId ? "border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100" : "border-slate-200"
              )}
              onClick={() => onClickProject(inboxId)}
              title="收件匣：未歸檔草稿集中處"
              aria-label="收件匣"
              type="button"
            >
              <div className={cx("flex items-center gap-2", focusMode ? "justify-center" : "")}
              >
                <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center">📥</div>
                {!focusMode ? (
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="font-semibold truncate">收件匣</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">未歸檔 {inboxCount} 筆</div>
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            </button>

            {!focusMode ? (
              <div className="mt-2">
                <Button
                  className="w-full"
                  size="md"
                  variant="success"
                  title="新增草稿到收件匣，並直接開啟右欄編輯"
                  ariaLabel="新增草稿"
                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: true })}
                >
                  ＋新增草稿
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          {/* Projects list */}
          <div className={cx("flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2", focusMode ? "p-2" : "")}
          >
            {projects.map((p) => {
              const active = selectedProjectId === p.id;
              return (
                <div key={p.id} className={cx("rounded-2xl border bg-white", active ? "border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100" : "border-slate-200")}>
                  <button
                    className="w-full p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 rounded-2xl transition-colors"
                    onClick={() => onClickProject(p.id)}
                    title="點擊切換專案（若列表已隱藏，點同一專案會叫回列表）"
                    aria-label={`專案：${p.name}`}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    <div className={cx("flex items-start gap-2", focusMode ? "justify-center" : "")}
                    >
                      <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center">📁</div>
                      {!focusMode ? (
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="truncate font-semibold">{p.name}</div>
                          <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 min-w-0">
                            <span className={cx("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border", chipTone(p.status))}>{p.status}</span>
                            <span className="truncate">進度：{p.progressDate || "—"}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ) : null}

                      {!focusMode ? (
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="ghost"
                          title="專案說明頁：連結/摘要/進度日期"
                          ariaLabel="專案說明"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            setSelectedProjectId(p.id);
                            openProjectDetails();
                          }}
                        >
                          ℹ️
                        </Button>
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* Divider: left */}
        {!focusMode ? (
          <div
            className="w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300"
            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider("left")}
            title="拖曳調整左欄寬度"
          />
        ) : null}

        {/* Middle column (prompt list) */}
        {effectiveListVisible ? (
          <main className="shrink-0 border-r bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden flex flex-col" style={{ width: midW }}>
            {/* Toolbar */}
            <div className="h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="font-semibold truncate">提示詞列表</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                  專案：{selectedProjectId === inboxId ? "收件匣" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="success"
                  title="新增提示詞：加入目前專案（或收件匣），並直接開啟右欄編輯"
                  ariaLabel="新增提示詞"
                  onClick={() => createPrompt({ toInbox: selectedProjectId === inboxId })}
                >
                  ＋新增
                </Button>
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="neutral"
                  title="清除搜尋與篩選條件"
                  ariaLabel="清除篩選"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSearch("");
                    setTypeFilter("all");
                    setStatusFilter("all");
                  }}
                >
                  清除
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Filters */}
            <div className="px-3 py-3 border-b bg-white">
              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="搜尋（標題/內容/標籤/備註）"
                className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]"
                title="搜尋範圍：標題、備註、標籤、正文"
              />
              <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <select value={typeFilter} onChange={(e) => setTypeFilter(e.target.value)} className="h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" title="依 type 篩選">
                  {uniqueTypes.map((t) => (
                    <option key={t} value={t}>
                      {t === "all" ? "類型：全部" : t}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
                <select value={statusFilter} onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)} className="h-11 px-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" title="依 status 篩選">
                  {uniqueStatuses.map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>
                      {s === "all" ? "狀態：全部" : s}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* List */}
            <div ref={listScrollRef} className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 space-y-2">
              {filteredPrompts.length === 0 ? <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 p-3">查無符合的提示詞。</div> : null}

              {filteredPrompts.map((p) => {
                const active = p.id === selectedPromptId;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={p.id}
                    className={cx(
                      "w-full text-left p-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors",
                      active ? "border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100" : "border-slate-200"
                    )}
                    onClick={() => selectPrompt(p.id)}
                    title="點擊開啟右欄編輯（Pin OFF 會自動收合列表）"
                    aria-label={`提示詞：${p.fm.title}`}
                    type="button"
                  >
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-3">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="truncate font-semibold">{p.fm.title}</div>
                        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
                          <span className={cx("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border", chipTone(p.fm.status))}>{p.fm.status}</span>
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700">{p.fm.model}</span>
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700">{p.fm.type}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">{formatUpdated(p.fm.updatedAt)}</div>
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="neutral"
                          title="更多（原型：未實作清單操作）"
                          ariaLabel="更多"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            setSnackbar({ message: "原型：列表『更多』未實作", undo: null });
                          }}
                        >
                          ⋯
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </main>
        ) : (
          // Small handle to reveal list
          <button
            className="w-4 shrink-0 bg-indigo-200/70 hover:bg-indigo-300 cursor-pointer relative"
            onClick={() => setListVisible(true)}
            title="顯示提示詞列表（小把手）"
            aria-label="顯示提示詞列表"
            type="button"
          >
            <div className="absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 left-0 w-full text-xs text-indigo-900 select-none">
              <div className="rotate-90 whitespace-nowrap">列表</div>
            </div>
          </button>
        )}

        {/* Divider: middle */}
        {!focusMode && effectiveListVisible ? (
          <div
            className="w-[6px] cursor-col-resize bg-slate-200/70 hover:bg-slate-300"
            onPointerDown={onPointerDownDivider("mid")}
            title="拖曳調整中欄寬度"
          />
        ) : null}

        {/* Right column (editor) */}
        <section className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative">
          {/* Editor top bar */}
          <div className="h-12 px-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex items-center gap-3">
              {!effectiveListVisible ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="neutral" title="叫回提示詞列表（等同點左欄同專案一次）" ariaLabel="叫回列表" onClick={() => setListVisible(true)}>
                  叫回列表
                </Button>
              ) : null}

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="font-semibold truncate" title={selectedPromptTitle}>
                  {selectedPromptTitle}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                  {selectedProjectId === inboxId ? "收件匣" : projects.find((p) => p.id === selectedProjectId)?.name}
                  {selectedPrompt ? ` · ${wordCount} words` : ""}
                </div>
              </div>

              <span className={cx("px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border text-xs", saveBadge.cls)} title="自動儲存狀態（原型）">
                {saveBadge.text}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" variant="neutral" title="前言：每筆提示詞獨立前言（預設收合）" ariaLabel="前言" onClick={() => setPrefaceOpen((v) => !v)} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                {prefaceOpen ? "收起前言" : "展開前言"}
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant={metaDrawerOpen ? "primary" : "neutral"} title="Metadata Drawer：title/type/status/tags…（右欄滑出）" ariaLabel="Metadata" onClick={toggleMetaDrawer} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                Metadata
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant={snippetsOpen ? "primary" : "neutral"} title="片語 Drawer：點擊插入游標位置（右欄滑出）" ariaLabel="片語" onClick={toggleSnippets} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                片語
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant="info" title="複製正文到剪貼簿" ariaLabel="複製" onClick={copyToClipboard} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                複製
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant="neutral" title="建立副本（另存為）" ariaLabel="複製副本" onClick={duplicatePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                副本
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant="neutral" title="移動到另一專案（示意）" ariaLabel="移動" onClick={movePromptMock} disabled={!selectedPrompt || selectedProjectId === inboxId}>
                移動
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant="warn" title="歸檔：移除目前提示詞（可 Undo；會自動開下一筆）" ariaLabel="歸檔" onClick={archivePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                歸檔
              </Button>

              <Button size="sm" variant="danger" title="刪除：移除目前提示詞（可 Undo；會自動開下一筆）" ariaLabel="刪除" onClick={deletePrompt} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                刪除
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Preface (collapsible) */}
          {prefaceOpen ? (
            <div className="px-3 py-3 border-b bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="font-semibold">前言（每筆提示詞獨立）</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">預設收合；展開後可編輯</div>
              </div>
              <textarea
                ref={prefaceRef}
                value={selectedPrompt?.preface ?? ""}
                onChange={(e) => setPromptPreface(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-2 w-full h-28 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3 text-[16px] leading-6"
                placeholder="可放固定背景/上下文（例如專案共通描述、角色設定）。"
                disabled={!selectedPrompt}
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {/* Editor */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 p-3">
            <div className="h-full rounded-3xl border bg-white overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
              <div className="h-10 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-slate-50">
                <div className="text-sm font-semibold">目前編輯中（單欄純編輯）</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">建議外接螢幕：1920×1440</div>
              </div>
              <textarea
                ref={editorRef}
                value={selectedPrompt?.body ?? ""}
                onChange={(e) => setPromptBody(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full h-[calc(100%-40px)] p-4 text-[16px] leading-7 font-mono outline-none"
                placeholder={selectedPrompt ? "在這裡輸入提示詞內容…" : "請先在左/中欄選取提示詞或新增。"}
                disabled={!selectedPrompt}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Metadata Drawer (inside right pane) */}
          <PaneRightDrawer title="Metadata" open={metaDrawerOpen} onClose={() => setMetaDrawerOpen(false)} width={460} zIndex={40}>
            {!selectedPrompt ? (
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">尚未選取提示詞。</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
                  <div className="font-semibold">按鈕作用</div>
                  <ul className="mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1">
                    <li>此 Drawer 用於編輯 frontmatter（title/type/status/model/tags…）。</li>
                    <li>你已確認：修改 title 需即時反映到右欄標題。</li>
                  </ul>
                </div>

                <Field label="title" hint="右欄標題即時更新">
                  <input value={selectedPrompt.fm.title} onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ title: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" />
                </Field>

                <Field label="project" hint="顯示用，不做跨專案同步">
                  <input value={selectedPrompt.fm.project} readOnly className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 text-[16px]" />
                </Field>

                <Field label="type">
                  <select value={selectedPrompt.fm.type} onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ type: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]">
                    {Array.from(new Set(["其他", "模板", "需求", "UIUX", "技術", ...(uniqueTypes.filter((t) => t !== "all") as string[])]))
                      .filter(Boolean)
                      .map((t) => (
                        <option key={t} value={t}>
                          {t}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>

                <Field label="status">
                  <select value={selectedPrompt.fm.status} onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ status: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]">
                    {Array.from(new Set(["使用中", "進行中", "封存", ...(uniqueStatuses.filter((s) => s !== "all") as string[])]))
                      .filter(Boolean)
                      .map((s) => (
                        <option key={s} value={s}>
                          {s}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>

                <Field label="model">
                  <input value={selectedPrompt.fm.model} onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ model: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" placeholder="例如 gpt-4o-mini" />
                </Field>

                <Field label="tags" hint="以逗號分隔">
                  <input
                    value={(selectedPrompt.fm.tags ?? []).join(", ")}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const tags = e.target.value
                        .split(",")
                        .map((x) => x.trim())
                        .filter(Boolean);
                      setPromptFM({ tags });
                    }}
                    className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]"
                    placeholder="例如 UIUX, 模板, SA"
                  />
                </Field>

                <Field label="note">
                  <input value={selectedPrompt.fm.note} onChange={(e) => setPromptFM({ note: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" />
                </Field>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">createdAt</div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-semibold break-all">{selectedPrompt.fm.createdAt}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">updatedAt</div>
                    <div className="text-sm font-semibold break-all">{selectedPrompt.fm.updatedAt}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900">
                  提示：你的 Markdown frontmatter 會是 YAML，例如：<br />
                  <code className="text-xs">title / project / type / status / model / tags / note / updatedAt / createdAt</code>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </PaneRightDrawer>

          {/* Snippets Drawer (inside right pane) */}
          <PaneRightDrawer title="常用片語剪貼簿" open={snippetsOpen} onClose={() => setSnippetsOpen(false)} width={420} zIndex={30}>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
                <div className="font-semibold">使用方式</div>
                <ul className="mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1">
                  <li>點擊片語 → 插入到正文游標位置</li>
                  <li>不影響左/中欄寬度（右欄內滑出）</li>
                </ul>
              </div>

              {DEMO_SNIPPETS.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.id} className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-3">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-semibold truncate">{s.title}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{s.tag}</div>
                    </div>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="primary" title="插入到游標位置" ariaLabel="插入" onClick={() => insertSnippet(s.text)} disabled={!selectedPrompt}>
                      插入
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-700 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{s.text}</div>
                </div>
              ))}

              <Button className="w-full" size="md" variant="neutral" title="新增片語（原型：未實作）" ariaLabel="新增片語" onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: "原型：新增片語未實作", undo: null })}>
                ＋新增片語（原型）
              </Button>
            </div>
          </PaneRightDrawer>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Modals */}
      <OverlayModal title="專案說明" open={projectDetailsOpen} onClose={() => setProjectDetailsOpen(false)}>
        {!selectedProject ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">未選取專案。</div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Field label="專案名稱">
                <input value={selectedProject.name} onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { name: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" />
              </Field>
              <Field label="進度日期" hint="用於快速查找進度">
                <input value={selectedProject.progressDate ?? ""} onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { progressDate: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD" />
              </Field>
            </div>

            <Field label="摘要" hint="可貼 ChatGPT/Gemini 簡短總結">
              <textarea value={selectedProject.summary ?? ""} onChange={(e) => updateProject(selectedProject.id, { summary: e.target.value })} className="w-full h-28 p-3 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px] leading-6" placeholder="例如：本週完成…下週要…" />
            </Field>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border p-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="font-semibold">連結（可貼對話連結）</div>
                <Button size="sm" variant="success" title="新增連結" ariaLabel="新增連結" onClick={() => addProjectLink(selectedProject.id)}>
                  ＋新增
                </Button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                {(selectedProject.links ?? []).length === 0 ? <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">尚無連結。</div> : null}
                {(selectedProject.links ?? []).map((l) => (
                  <div key={l.id} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-center">
                    <input value={l.title} onChange={(e) => updateProjectLink(selectedProject.id, l.id, { title: e.target.value })} className="h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" placeholder="連結名稱" />
                    <input value={l.url} onChange={(e) => updateProjectLink(selectedProject.id, l.id, { url: e.target.value })} className="h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white text-[16px]" placeholder="https://..." />
                    <Button size="icon" variant="danger" title="刪除連結" ariaLabel="刪除連結" onClick={() => removeProjectLink(selectedProject.id, l.id)}>
                      🗑
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700">提示：你可以把「對話連結 + 摘要 + 進度日期」集中在這裡，方便追蹤目前專案執行狀況。</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </OverlayModal>

      <OverlayModal title="按鈕說明（Help）" open={helpOpen} onClose={() => setHelpOpen(false)} maxWidth={980}>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-slate-50 p-3 text-sm text-slate-700">
            <div className="font-semibold">核心動線</div>
            <ol className="mt-2 list-decimal pl-5 space-y-1">
              <li>左欄選專案（再點同專案一次可叫回列表）</li>
              <li>中欄選提示詞 → 右欄編輯（Pin OFF 會自動收合列表）</li>
              <li>右欄：展開前言／開 Metadata／插入片語／複製／副本／移動／歸檔／刪除</li>
            </ol>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border p-3">
              <div className="font-semibold">頂部按鈕</div>
              <ul className="mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
                <li>專注編輯：隱藏列表，最大化右欄編輯區</li>
                <li>專案說明：開啟專案說明頁（連結/摘要/進度日期）</li>
                <li>片語：開啟右欄片語抽屜（點插入游標位置）</li>
                <li>📌 釘住：全域開關；未釘住時選取提示詞會自動收合列表</li>
                <li>?：顯示本頁說明</li>
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-2xl border p-3">
              <div className="font-semibold">右欄按鈕</div>
              <ul className="mt-2 list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
                <li>展開/收起前言：每筆提示詞獨立前言（預設收合）</li>
                <li>Metadata：右欄內滑出 frontmatter 編輯抽屜（title 即時反映到右欄標題）</li>
                <li>片語：右欄內第二個抽屜（更省高度，維持編輯節奏）</li>
                <li>複製：複製正文到剪貼簿</li>
                <li>副本：建立副本並切換到副本（另存為）</li>
                <li>移動：移動到其他專案（原型示意）</li>
                <li>歸檔／刪除：移除後自動開下一筆；並提供 Undo</li>
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-amber-50 p-3 text-sm text-amber-900">目前為 UI 原型：新增專案、列表更多、片語新增 等功能先保留為示意，方便你後續交給 AI Coding 工具逐步補齊。</div>
        </div>
      </OverlayModal>

      <Snackbar state={snackbar} onClose={() => setSnackbar(null)} />
    </div>
  );
}

## 3) `src/utils/helpers.ts`

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function cx(...args: Array<string | false | null | undefined>) {
  return args.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

export function useLocalStorageState<T>(key: string, initial: T) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
      if (raw == null) return initial;
      return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
    } catch {
      return initial;
    }
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [key, value]);

  return [value, setValue] as const;
}

export function formatUpdated(iso: string) {
  // 顯示「YYYY-MM-DD」
  try {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    const yyyy = d.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
  } catch {
    return iso;
  }
}

let _idSeq = 1;
export function nextId(prefix: string) {
  _idSeq += 1;
  return `${prefix}_${Date.now()}_${_idSeq}`;
}

export function chipTone(status: string) {
  // 用 Tailwind 類別表達色系（依你目前原型習慣）
  if (status.includes("使用")) return "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800";
  if (status.includes("進行")) return "border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800";
  if (status.includes("封存") || status.includes("歸檔")) return "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700";
  return "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-700";
}
```

```ts
import type { Project, PromptItem, SnippetItem, Frontmatter } from "../types";

export function makeFrontmatter(partial: Partial<Frontmatter>): Frontmatter {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    title: partial.title ?? "未命名",
    project: partial.project ?? "",
    type: partial.type ?? "其他",
    status: partial.status ?? "使用中",
    model: partial.model ?? "gpt-4o-mini",
    tags: partial.tags ?? [],
    note: partial.note ?? "",
    createdAt: partial.createdAt ?? now,
    updatedAt: partial.updatedAt ?? now,
  };
}
```

## 5) `src/components/ui/Button.tsx`

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { cx } from "../../utils/helpers";

type Variant = "primary" | "neutral" | "ghost" | "danger" | "warn" | "success" | "info";
type Size = "sm" | "md" | "icon";

const VARIANT: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700",
  neutral: "bg-white text-slate-800 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50",
  ghost: "bg-transparent text-slate-700 border-transparent hover:bg-slate-100",
  danger: "bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700",
  warn: "bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600",
  success: "bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700",
  info: "bg-sky-600 text-white border-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-700",
};

const SIZE: Record<Size, string> = {
  sm: "h-9 px-3 rounded-2xl text-sm",
  md: "h-11 px-4 rounded-2xl text-[16px]",
  icon: "h-10 w-10 rounded-2xl text-[16px] grid place-items-center",
};

export function Button(props: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onClick?: (e: any) => void;
  className?: string;
  size?: Size;
  variant?: Variant;
  title?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}) {
  const { children, onClick, className, size = "md", variant = "neutral", title, ariaLabel, disabled } = props;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      disabled={disabled}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      title={title}
      onClick={disabled ? undefined : onClick}
      className={cx(
        "inline-flex items-center justify-center border transition-colors select-none",
        VARIANT[variant],
        SIZE[size],
        disabled ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "",
        className
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import React from "react";
import { Button } from "./Button";
import { cx } from "../../utils/helpers";

export function PaneRightDrawer(props: {
  title: string;
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  width?: number;
  zIndex?: number;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const { title, open, onClose, width = 420, zIndex = 30, children } = props;

  return (
    <div
      className={cx("absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none")}
      style={{ zIndex }}
      aria-hidden={!open}
    >
      {/* overlay */}
      <div
        className={cx("absolute inset-0 bg-black/20 transition-opacity", open ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0")}
        style={{ pointerEvents: open ? "auto" : "none" }}
        onClick={onClose}
      />
      {/* panel */}
      <div
        className={cx("absolute top-0 right-0 h-full bg-white border-l shadow-xl transition-transform")}
        style={{
          width,
          transform: open ? "translateX(0)" : `translateX(${width}px)`,
          pointerEvents: open ? "auto" : "none",
        }}
      >
        <div className="h-12 px-3 border-b flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
          <div className="font-semibold">{title}</div>
          <Button size="icon" variant="neutral" ariaLabel="關閉" title="關閉" onClick={onClose}>
            ✕
          </Button>
        </div>
        <div className="p-3 overflow-auto h-[calc(100%-48px)]">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 8) `src/components/ui/OverlayModal.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect } from "react";
import { Button } from "./Button";

export function OverlayModal(props: {
  title: string;
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  maxWidth?: number;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const { title, open, onClose, maxWidth = 860, children } = props;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[90]">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="w-full rounded-3xl bg-white border shadow-xl overflow-hidden" style={{ maxWidth }}>
          <div className="h-12 px-4 border-b flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
            <div className="font-semibold">{title}</div>
            <Button size="icon" variant="neutral" ariaLabel="關閉" title="關閉" onClick={onClose}>
              ✕
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 max-h-[78vh] overflow-auto">{children}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## 9) `src/components/ui/Snackbar.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect } from "react";
import { Button } from "./Button";
import type { SnackbarState } from "../../types";

export function Snackbar(props: { state: SnackbarState | null; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { state, onClose } = props;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state) return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => onClose(), 3500);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [state, onClose]);

  if (!state) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[95]">
      <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white shadow-lg px-4 py-3 flex items-center gap-3 min-w-[320px]">
        <div className="text-sm text-slate-800">{state.message}</div>
        <div className="flex-1" />
        {state.undo ? (
          <Button size="sm" variant="neutral" ariaLabel="Undo" title="Undo" onClick={() => { state.undo?.(); onClose(); }}>
            Undo
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" ariaLabel="關閉" title="關閉" onClick={onClose}>
          ✕
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
```

## 10) `src/selfTests.ts`

> 這份會在 `App.tsx` mount 時跑一次（console.assert），不需要測試框架；你原本的測試風格我保留，並補了幾個不會影響功能的輕量檢查。

```ts
import { chipTone, formatUpdated, nextId } from "./utils/helpers";

export function runSelfTests() {
  try {
    // formatUpdated
    const d = formatUpdated("2025-12-24T00:00:00.000Z");
    console.assert(d === "2025-12-24", "formatUpdated should format to YYYY-MM-DD");

    // nextId uniqueness-ish
    const a = nextId("t");
    const b = nextId("t");
    console.assert(a !== b, "nextId should differ");

    // chipTone returns a non-empty string
    console.assert(chipTone("使用中").length > 0, "chipTone should return classes");
    console.assert(chipTone("進行中").includes("sky") || chipTone("進行中").includes("bg-"), "chipTone should map status");

    // No throw
    console.log("[selfTests] ok");
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[selfTests] failed", e);
  }
}
```
