[];\n        return { ...prev, [inboxId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n      });\n      setSelectedProjectId(inboxId);\n      setSelectedPromptId(id);\n      setPrefaceOpen(false);\n      setListVisible(pinList ? true : false);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增草稿到收件匣（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [newPrompt, ...list] };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已新增提示詞（已開啟編輯）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const duplicatePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const id = nextId(\"dup\");\n    const copy: PromptItem = {\n      ...selectedPrompt,\n      id,\n      fm: {\n        ...selectedPrompt.fm,\n        title: `${selectedPrompt.fm.title}（複製）`,\n        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      },\n    };\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [copy, ...list] };\n    });\n    setSelectedPromptId(id);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n    if (!pinList) setListVisible(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({ message: \"已建立副本（另存為）\", undo: null });\n  };\n\n  const pickNextAfterRemove = (removedId: string) => {\n    const currentList = promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n    const idx = currentList.findIndex((p) => p.id === removedId);\n    return currentList[idx + 1] ?? currentList[idx - 1] ?? null;\n  };\n\n  const archivePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n    const next = pickNextAfterRemove(removed.id);\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已歸檔：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const deletePrompt = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const removed = selectedPrompt;\n    const next = pickNextAfterRemove(removed.id);\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n      return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: list.filter((p) => p.id !== removed.id) };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已刪除：${removed.fm.title}（已自動切換下一筆）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const list = prev[selectedProjectId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [selectedProjectId]: [removed, ...list] };\n        });\n        setSelectedPromptId(removed.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const movePromptMock = () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    const target = projects.find((p) => p.id !== selectedProjectId && p.id !== inboxId);\n    if (!target) return;\n\n    const fromId = selectedProjectId;\n    const toId = target.id;\n    const moved = selectedPrompt;\n    const next = pickNextAfterRemove(moved.id);\n\n    setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n      const fromList = (prev[fromId] ?? []).filter((p) => p.id !== moved.id);\n      const toList = prev[toId] ?? [];\n      const moved2: PromptItem = {\n        ...moved,\n        fm: { ...moved.fm, project: target.name, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },\n      };\n      return { ...prev, [fromId]: fromList, [toId]: [moved2, ...toList] };\n    });\n\n    setSelectedPromptId(next?.id ?? null);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n\n    setSnackbar({\n      message: `已移動到：${target.name}（示意）`,\n      undo: () => {\n        setPromptsByProject((prev) => {\n          const toList = (prev[toId] ?? [];\n          return { ...prev, [toId]: toList, [fromId]: [moved, ...fromList] };\n        });\n        setSelectedProjectId(fromId);\n        setSelectedPromptId(moved.id);\n      },\n    });\n  };\n\n  const copyToClipboard = async () => {\n    if (!selectedPrompt) return;\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedPrompt.body);\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"已複製到剪貼簿\", undo: null });\n    } catch {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"複製失敗：瀏覽器限制\", undo: null });\n    }\n  };\n\n  const insertSnippet = (snippetText: string) => {\n    const el = editorRef.current;\n    if (!el || !selectedPrompt) return;\n    const start = el.selectionStart ?? 0;\n    const end = el.selectionEnd ?? 0;\n    const before = selectedPrompt.body.slice(0, start);\n    const after = selectedPrompt.body.slice(end);\n    const nextText = before + snippetText + after;\n    setPromptBody(nextText);\n\n    requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n      const pos = start + snippetText.length;\n      el.focus();\n      el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);\n    });\n  };\n\n  // Project details (edit fields)\n  const updateProject = (pid: string, patch: Partial<Project>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === pid ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));\n  };\n\n  const addProjectLink = (pid: string) => {\n    const id = nextId(\"link\");\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) =>\n        p.id === pid\n          ? {\n              ...p,\n              links: [...(p.links ?? []), { id, title: \"新連結\", url: \"\" }],\n            }\n          : p\n      )\n    );\n  };\n\n  const updateProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string, patch: Partial<LinkItem>) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).map((l) => (l.id === lid ? { ...l, ...patch } : l));\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  const removeProjectLink = (pid: string, lid: string) => {\n    setProjects((prev) =>\n      prev.map((p) => {\n        if (p.id !== pid) return p;\n        const links = (p.links ?? []).filter((l) => l.id !== lid);\n        return { ...p, links };\n      })\n    );\n  };\n\n  // Resizer logic\n  const dragState = useRef<{ dragging: null | \"left\" | \"mid\"; startX: number; startLeft: number; startMid: number }>({\n    dragging: null,\n    startX: 0,\n    startLeft: 0,\n    startMid: 0,\n  });\n\n  const onPointerDownDivider = (which: \"left\" | \"mid\") => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    dragState.current.dragging = which;\n    dragState.current.startX = e.clientX;\n    dragState.current.startLeft = leftW;\n    dragState.current.startMid = midW;\n    if (e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture) e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      const ds = dragState.current;\n      if (!ds.dragging) return;\n      const dx = e.clientX - ds.startX;\n\n      if (ds.dragging === \"left\") {\n        setLeftW(clamp(ds.startLeft + dx, 280, 520));\n      }\n      if (ds.dragging === \"mid\") {\n        setMidW(clamp(ds.startMid + dx, 360, 720));\n      }\n    };\n\n    const onPointerUp = () => {\n      dragState.current.dragging = null;\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", onPointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener(\"pointerup\", onPointerUp);\n    };\n  }, [leftW, midW, setLeftW, setMidW]);\n\n  // Middle list scroll persistence\n  const listScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const listScrollPos = useRef<number>(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) listScrollPos.current = el.scrollTop;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (listVisible) {\n      const el = listScrollRef.current;\n      if (el) el.scrollTop = listScrollPos.current;\n    }\n  }, [listVisible]);\n\n  // Hide list if focus mode\n  const effectiveListVisible = !focusMode && listVisible;\n\n  // UI text size (+2px)\n  const rootText = \"text-[18px] leading-7\";\n\n  const openProjectDetails = () => {\n    if (!selectedProject || selectedProjectId === inboxId) {\n      setSnackbar({ message: \"收件匣不需要專案說明頁\", undo: null });\n      return;\n    }\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const openHelp = () => {\n    closeAllOverlays();\n    setHelpOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const toggleSnippets = () => {\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen((v) => !v);\n  };\n\n  const toggleMetaDrawer = () => {\n    setHelpOpen(false);\n    setProjectDetailsOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setMetaDrawerOpen((v) => !v);\n  };\n\n  // When switching prompt, close drawers that might confuse\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setMetaDrawerOpen(false);\n    setSnippetsOpen(false);\n    setPrefaceOpen(false);\n  }, [selectedPromptId, selectedProjectId]);\n\n  // Self tests (lightweight)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    try {\n      const id1 = nextId(\"t\");\n      const id2 = nextId(\"t\");\n      console.assert(id1 !== id2, \"nextId should generate different values\");\n      console.assert(chipTone(\"使用中\").includes(\"emerald\"), \"chipTone should map 使用中 to emerald\");\n      console.assert(formatUpdated(new Date().toISOString()).includes(\"/\"), \"formatUpdated should produce MM/DD\");\n      // Added test: filter stability\n      const sample = (promptsByProject[selectedProjectId] ?? [])[0];\n      if (sample) {\n        console.assert(typeof sample.fm.title === \"string\", \"prompt frontmatter title should be string\");\n      }\n    } catch {\n      // ignore\n    }\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, []);\n\n  const saveBadge = useMemo(() => {\n    if (saveState === \"saved\") return { text: \"已儲存\", cls: \"bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200\" };\n    if (saveState === \"saving\") return { text: \"儲存中…\", cls: \"bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border-sky-200\" };\n    return { text: \"未儲存\", cls: \"bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200\" };\n  }, [saveState]);\n\n  const wordCount = useMemo(() => {\n    const s = selectedPrompt?.body ?? \"\";\n    const trimmed = s.trim();\n    if (!trimmed) return 0;\n    return trimmed.split(/\\s+/g).length;\n  }, [selectedPrompt?.body]);\n\n  // -----------------------\n  // Render\n  // -----------------------\n\n  return (\n    <div className={cx(\"h-screen w-full bg-slate-100\", rootText)}>\n      {/* Top Header */}\n      <div className=\"h-14 shrink-0 px-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b bg-white\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0\">\n          <div className=\"h-9 w-9 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900\" />\n          <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n            <div className=\"font-semibold truncate\">Prompt Management Workspace</div>\n            <div className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate\">專案 × 提示詞 × 編輯（Pin、Drawer、Undo）</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <Button\n            size=\"md\"\n            variant={focusMode ? (\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <div className=\"font-semibold\">專案</div>\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">（含進度日期）</span>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"font-semibold\">P</div>\n            )}\n\n            {!focusMode ? (\n              <Button\n                size=\"icon\"\n                variant=\"neutral\"\n                title=\"新增專案（原型：未實作）\"\n                ariaLabel=\"新增專案\"\n                onClick={() => setSnackbar({ message: \"原型：新增專案未實作\", undo: null })}\n              >\n                ＋\n              </Button>\n            ) : null}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Inbox */}\n          <div className={cx(\"px-3 py-3 border-b\", focusMode ? \"px-2\" : \"\")}> \n            <button\n              className={cx(\n                \"w-full rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors\",\n                selectedProjectId === inboxId ? \"border-indigo-600 ring-2 ring-indigo-100\" : \"border-slate-200\"\n              )}\n              onClick={() => onClickProject(inboxId)}\n              title=\"收件匣：未歸檔草稿集中處\"\n              aria-label=\"收件匣\"\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              <div className={cx(\"flex items-center gap-2\", focusMode ? \"justify-center\" : \"\")}> \n                <div className=\"h-8 w-8 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center\">📥</div>\n                {!focusMode ?    - Pin 全域開關（Pin OFF：選取 prompt 後自動收合中欄）

你這個錯誤 **`Unterminated JSX contents`** 幾乎都是因為：在某一行 JSX 內部（通常是 `<button> ...` 或 `<div> ...`）**少了對應的 `</...>` 結尾**，或是中途被截斷，導致解析器一路讀到檔案尾巴還沒結束。

你目前的 `index.tsx` 在 **第 1214 行附近剛好卡在 `<button ...>`**，而且 canvas 裡的原始檔也確實在那邊被截斷，所以就會噴這個錯。

我已經直接幫你「止血」處理，避免再被單一超長檔案卡死：

## 我做了什麼調整（已完成）
1. **把 `index.tsx` 改成極小的 entry**：只負責 `return <App />;`
2. **把原本超長的 App UI 原型移到 `App.tsx`**
3. 同時補齊拆檔所需的：
   - `lib/types.ts`
